# BEVFusion 体素化算子 SYCL 移植 — 工作说明与使用指南

> 本文件说明：做了什么、文件夹里是什么、怎么编译运行。

## 1. 这是什么

把 NVIDIA BEVFusion 的 PointPillars 激光雷达体素化 CUDA 算子（hash-table + scatter + reduce_mean，half 精度，XYZ/ZYX 坐标序）移植到 Intel SYCL，在 Intel Arc Graphics [0xb080] GPU 上跑通并优化。

- **平台**：Intel Arc Graphics [0xb080] (B390, ~29GB)，oneAPI 2025.3.3，OpenCL 后端。
- **源算子**：CUDA-BEVFusion 的 `lidar-voxelization.cu`。
- **工作目录**：`/work/bevfusion_migration/`。

## 2. 做了什么

1. **移植**：CUDA → SYCL，忠实保留 half 精度 + XYZ/ZYX 坐标序 + hash-table 开放寻址算法。3 个 kernel：`build_hash_table`（插点入 hash 表）/ `voxelization`（lookup + scatter 点特征）/ `reduce_mean`（每体素求均值输出 half）。
2. **正确性验证**：合成小数据逐 bit 对 CPU 参考（`ALL TESTS PASSED`）+ 真实 24 万点 ±1 邻接边界分析（`REAL-DATA TEST PASSED`）。
3. **性能对比**：同平台对比 hash-table 版 vs Intel dense 版 → BEVFusion 细参数下 dense OOM，只有 hash-table 能跑。
4. **性能优化**：4 项优化（relaxed 原子 / 去 host sync / 去 memset / reduce_mean 寄存器累加），累计 wall **3.5-3.8×**（240k 真实点云 1.938→0.530ms）。
5. **性能极限**：瓶颈 voxelization 是 memory-latency-bound（hash 表随机访存，算法固有），不改算法已近实用极限。

## 3. 目录结构

```
/work/bevfusion_migration/
├── README.md                          # 本文件（工作说明 + 使用指南）
├── build.sh                           # 编译脚本（编 6 个目标）
├── common/                            # 共享头文件
│   ├── launch.hpp                     # linear_launch + 原子序开关 + profiling 支持
│   ├── dtype.hpp                      # nvtype::half 等类型别名（对齐 NVIDIA API）
│   └── check.hpp                      # 校验/断言宏
├── cvcuda_ops/                        # 下一个算子移植预留目录（空，勿删）
├── lidar_voxelization/
│   ├── voxelizer.cpp                  # 核心 SYCL 体素化算子（opt4 累计版）
│   ├── voxelizer.hpp                  # 接口声明 + last_profile() 计时接口
│   ├── dense_ref/                     # B 路对比用：Intel dense 参考实现
│   │   ├── pointpillars/voxelizer.{cpp,hpp}
│   │   └── common/voxelizer_utils.hpp
│   └── test/
│       ├── correctness/               # 合成正确性测试
│       │   ├── test_voxelizer.cpp     # 源码
│       │   └── test_voxelizer         # 编译后二进制（无参数）
│       ├── real/                      # 真实点云正确性 + 性能测试
│       │   ├── test_voxelizer_real.cpp
│       │   ├── test_voxelizer_real         # baseline (seq_cst，优化前)
│       │   └── test_voxelizer_real_relaxed # opt4 (relaxed，优化后，日常用)
│       ├── profile/                   # C 路逐 kernel profiling 测试
│       │   ├── test_voxelizer_profile.cpp
│       │   ├── test_voxelizer_profile         # baseline (seq_cst)
│       │   └── test_voxelizer_profile_relaxed # opt4 (relaxed)
│       ├── bench_compare/             # B 路 hash vs dense 对比测试
│       │   ├── test_bench_compare.cpp
│       │   └── test_bench_compare     # 编译后二进制
│       └── data/
│           ├── real/                  # 真实点云（nuScenes .bin，[N,5] float32）
│           │   ├── 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin   # 240k 点
│           │   └── 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b.bin   # 267k 点
│           └── synthetic/             # 合成点云（均匀随机，最坏原子竞争负载）
│               ├── synth_50000.bin
│               ├── synth_100000.bin
│               ├── synth_500000.bin
│               └── synth_1000000.bin
└── test_intel_env/                    # 早期 oneAPI 学习实验（与体素化无关，环境验证用）
    ├── test.cpp / test
    ├── test_buffer.cpp / test_buffer / test_buffer_debug
    └── vector-add-usm.cpp / vector-add-usm / vector-add-host.cpp
```

**组织原则**：每个测试文件夹里源码 `.cpp` 和编译后的二进制放一起；点云按真实/合成分开；早期学习实验单独隔离在 `test_intel_env/`。

## 4. 怎么编译

宿主机有 `icpx`（容器里没有，**必须宿主机编译**）：

```bash
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
bash build.sh
```

编出 6 个二进制，自动放到对应 `test/` 子文件夹。默认 4 个目标是 seq_cst baseline，2 个 `_relaxed` 目标加 `-DVOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS`（opt4）。

## 5. 怎么运行

> **必须**带 `ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu` —— Level Zero 后端在这台机器有 bug 会段错误。
> 建议每个 SSH session 先 export 一次，后面就不用每次写了：
> ```bash
> export ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu
> ```

下面命令都假设你在 `/work/bevfusion_migration` 根目录（注意路径前缀）。

### 5.1 合成正确性测试（无参数，自己造点）
```bash
./lidar_voxelization/test/correctness/test_voxelizer
```
预期最后一行：`=== ALL TESTS PASSED ===`。

### 5.2 真实点云测试（要给 .bin 路径）
```bash
# opt4 版（快，日常用这个）
./lidar_voxelization/test/real/test_voxelizer_real_relaxed \
  lidar_voxelization/test/data/real/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin

# baseline 版（A/B 对比用）
./lidar_voxelization/test/real/test_voxelizer_real \
  lidar_voxelization/test/data/real/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin
```
可选第二参数 `runs`（默认 10）。预期：`=== REAL-DATA TEST PASSED ===`，并报 best/avg wall ms。

### 5.3 profiling（逐 kernel 计时，要 `VOX_PROFILE=1`）
```bash
# opt4
VOX_PROFILE=1 ./lidar_voxelization/test/profile/test_voxelizer_profile_relaxed \
  lidar_voxelization/test/data/real/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin 20 XYZ

# baseline
VOX_PROFILE=1 ./lidar_voxelization/test/profile/test_voxelizer_profile \
  lidar_voxelization/test/data/real/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin 20 XYZ
```
参数：`<points.bin> [runs=20] [XYZ|ZYX]`。输出 3 个 kernel（build_hash / voxelization / reduce_mean）各自的 best/avg ms + 占比 + kernel 之和 + wall + overhead + 吞吐。

> `VOX_PROFILE=1` 不开的话，queue 不记 event 时间戳，`last_profile()` 读不到 kernel 时间（只能测总 wall）。

### 5.4 B 路对比（hash vs dense）
```bash
./lidar_voxelization/test/bench_compare/test_bench_compare \
  lidar_voxelization/test/data/real/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin
```
> 注意：dense 只在粗参数（CenterPoint）下能跑；BEVFusion 细参数下 dense 会 OOM，只有 hash-table 能跑。

## 6. baseline vs `_relaxed` 的区别

同一个源码，编译选项不同：

| 二进制 | 编译选项 | 原子序 | 是哪个版本 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `test_voxelizer_real` / `test_voxelizer_profile` | 默认 | seq_cst | **baseline 优化前**（A/B 的 A 端） |
| `test_voxelizer_real_relaxed` / `test_voxelizer_profile_relaxed` | `-DVOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS` | relaxed | **opt4 优化后**（最终最优版，日常用） |

`_relaxed` 不只是"relaxed 原子"——它是 opt1(relaxed)+opt2(去sync)+opt3(去memset)+opt4(寄存器累加) 四项优化的总和。名字叫 `_relaxed` 只因为 relaxed 是唯一一个用编译开关控制的优化，拿它当后缀。`test_voxelizer`（合成）和 `test_bench_compare` 没有 `_relaxed` 变体，因为它们不做 A/B。

## 7. 性能报告

> 测量方法：逐 kernel 用 SYCL event profiling（`VOX_PROFILE=1`）取 device 时间；关键 A/B 对比用 controlled A/B（同一 SSH session 交替跑 baseline / opt4 各多轮取 best），避免 Arc GPU 跨进程 ±10%+ 时钟/热噪声。
> BEVFusion 细参数：min/max_range=(-54,-54,-5)/(54,54,3)，voxel_size=(0.075,0.075,0.2)，grid=1440×1440×40，max_voxels=160000，max_points_per_voxel=10，num_feature=5。

### 7.1 B 路：hash-table（本移植）vs dense（Intel 容器参考）

同进程、同 Arc GPU、同点云、同参数对比（CenterPoint 粗参数 0.8/0.8/8.0，grid 128×128×1，max_voxels 12000——dense 在此才能跑）：

| 点云 | [A] hash-table（本移植） | [B] dense（Intel） | voxel 数 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 50k 合成 | 1.56 ms / 32 Mpts/s | 0.44 ms / 113 | 8141≈8146 |
| 100k 合成 | 0.75 ms / 133 | 0.41 ms / 245 | 8661≈8659 |
| 500k 合成 | 2.18 ms / 230 | 0.66 ms / 761 | 8704=8704 |
| 1M 合成 | 4.02 ms / 249 | 0.98 ms / 1018 | 8704=8704 |
| 240k 真实 | 0.86 ms / 279 | 0.51 ms / 466 | 2252≈2250 |

- voxel 数一致 → 公平性验证通过。
- 粗参数下 dense 快 2~4×：grid 极小（16k cell），dense 直接 scatter 开销小；hash-table 有原子竞争 + 探测开销。
- **BEVFusion 细参数下 dense OOM（16.6GB > 15GB），只有 hash-table 能跑**。hash-table 内存绑定 num_points（细参数 ~20MB），dense 绑定 grid 大小（细参数 8300 万 cell）。所以本移植的价值是"细参数下能用"，不是"粗参数下更快"。

### 7.2 C 路：优化项

| 优化 | 改动 | 收益 |
|---|---|---|
| opt1 relaxed 原子 | 原子序 seq_cst → relaxed（insert/lookup 在不同 kernel，由 in-order queue + 中间 memcpy 序列化，无跨 kernel 竞争，relaxed 安全） | kernel 3.1× |
| opt2 去 host sync | 去掉 forward() 中段 `q.wait()`+读计数，reduce_mean 改按 max_voxels launch + kernel 自守 | wall −7~17% |
| opt3 去 memset | 砍 voxels_temp 32MB 防御性 0xff memset（读的槽全由 voxelization 写过） | wall −4~36% |
| opt4 寄存器累加 | reduce_mean 从全局 RMW 改寄存器累加器 `acc[16]` 单遍顺序读 | reduce_mean 2.2×，wall −22% |

四项叠加，opt4 = opt1+2+3+4 全开（即 `_relaxed` 二进制）。

### 7.3 累计优化结果（best wall ms，BEVFusion 细参数 XYZ）

| 点云 | baseline (seq_cst) | opt1 relaxed | opt2 +nosync | opt3 +nomemset | opt4 +reducemean | 累计加速 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 240k 真实 | 1.938 | 0.865 | 0.798 | 0.705 | **0.530** | **3.66×** |
| 267k 真实 | 2.305 | 0.969 | 0.902 | 0.865 | **0.666** | **3.46×** |
| 50k 合成 | 0.781 | 0.456 | 0.377 | 0.241 | **0.204** | 3.83× |
| 100k 合成 | 1.319 | 0.664 | 0.626 | 0.480 | **0.352** | 3.75× |

吞吐（kernel sum）：240k 149→587 Mpts/s（3.9×），267k 136→486 Mpts/s。

### 7.4 opt4 per-kernel 分解（best ms，GPU warm）

| 点云 | build_hash | voxelization | reduce_mean | kernel sum | wall | overhead |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 240k 真实 | 0.055 (13%) | 0.271 (66%) | 0.083 (20%) | 0.409 | 0.530 | 0.121 |
| 267k 真实 | 0.066 (12%) | 0.356 (65%) | 0.127 (23%) | 0.549 | 0.666 | 0.117 |
| 50k 合成 | 0.023 (21%) | 0.071 (67%) | 0.013 (12%) | 0.107 | 0.204 | 0.098 |
| 100k 合成 | 0.038 (15%) | 0.156 (61%) | 0.060 (24%) | 0.255 | 0.352 | 0.098 |

### 7.5 性能极限分析

- opt4 后 **voxelization 占 kernel sum ~65%**，是瓶颈。
- voxelization 是 **memory-latency-bound，非带宽饱和**：240k 点访存量 ~12-15MB / 0.271ms ≈ 50 GB/s，远低于 Arc 峰值带宽；瓶颈是 hash_table / voxels_temp / num_points_per_voxel 的**随机访问 cache miss 延迟**，是 hash-table 算法固有（每点 lookup 一次 hash 表 + 按 voxel_id 随机散落写）。不改算法（点按 voxel 预排序等）无法显著降低。
- overhead（wall − kernel sum）稳定 ~0.10-0.12ms，主因 hash_table 4.8MB 0xff memset（empty_key 标记，insert 必需）+ 3 次 kernel launch + 最终 wait，基本不可减。
- **结论**：不改 hash-table 算法，opt4（3.5-3.8×）已接近该算法在 Arc GPU 上的实用性能极限。进一步提速需算法级改动（点排序 / 分块 / 不同数据结构），属另一阶段工作。

### 7.6 正确性

- 合成小数据：XYZ/ZYX feature 均值 vs CPU 参考（half 容差 1e-2）→ `ALL TESTS PASSED`。
- 真实点云（240k/267k）：voxel 数偏差 <0.05%，不一致处 99.8-100% 为 ±1 单 voxel 边界平移，根因是 Intel GPU float 除法非 IEEE 正确舍入（**设备固有特性，非 port bug**）→ `REAL-DATA TEST PASSED`。
- opt1-4 正确性与 baseline 完全一致（不漏点/不漏体素，voxel 数、边界平移率、feature 均值均不变）。

## 8. 注意事项

- **必须用 OpenCL 后端**：`ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu`。默认 Level Zero 后端在这台机器跑 kernel 会段错误。
- **profiling 要开 `VOX_PROFILE=1`**：否则读不到 per-kernel 时间。
- **正确性容差**：真实点云有 ~0.04% 体素数偏差，是 Intel GPU 浮点除法非 IEEE 正确舍入导致的边界体素 ±1 平移（**设备固有特性，非 bug**），测试用 ±1 邻接分析区分设备特性 vs 真 bug。合成小数据是逐 bit 精确的。
- **测量噪声**：Arc GPU 跨进程有 ±10%+ 时钟/热噪声。重要 A/B 对比要在**同一 SSH session 交替跑** baseline / opt4 各多轮，各取 best 比（不要先跑完 A 退出再跑 B）。
- **`cvcuda_ops/` 为下一个算子移植预留，不要删除。**
- **`test_intel_env/` 是早期 oneAPI 学习实验**（vector-add / buffer 冒烟测试），与体素化无关，环境验证用，可忽略。

### common/check.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/check.hpp
// SYCL port: Assert* macros kept (pure C++). CUDA checkRuntime/check_runtime
// removed — SYCL reports errors via sycl::exception, handled by try/catch.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __CHECK_HPP__
#define __CHECK_HPP__

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

namespace nv {

#define NVUNUSED2(a, b) \
  {                     \
    (void)(a);          \
    (void)(b);          \
  }
#define NVUNUSED(a) \
  { (void)(a); }

#define Assertf(cond, fmt, ...)                                                                                         \
  do {                                                                                                                  \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                                      \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " fmt "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__); \
      abort();                                                                                                          \
    }                                                                                                                   \
  } while (false)

#define Asserts(cond, s)                                                                                 \
  do {                                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " s "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                                           \
    }                                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

#define Assert(cond)                                                                     \
  do {                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                           \
    }                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

};  // namespace nv

#endif  // #ifndef __CHECK_HPP__
```

### common/dtype.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/dtype.hpp
// SYCL port: POD structs kept identical; half now aliases sycl::half.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __DTYPE_HPP__
#define __DTYPE_HPP__

#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace nvtype {

struct Int2 {
  int x, y;
  Int2() = default;
  Int2(int x, int y = 0) : x(x), y(y) {}
};

struct Int3 {
  int x, y, z;
  Int3() = default;
  Int3(int x, int y = 0, int z = 0) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Float2 {
  float x, y;
  Float2() = default;
  Float2(float x, float y = 0) : x(x), y(y) {}
};

struct Float3 {
  float x, y, z;
  Float3() = default;
  Float3(float x, float y = 0, float z = 0) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Float4 {
  float x, y, z, w;
  Float4() = default;
  Float4(float x, float y = 0, float z = 0, float w = 0) : x(x), y(y), z(z), w(w) {}
};

// Original nvtype::half was an opaque type tag; real math used cuda_fp16.
// In SYCL we map it directly to sycl::half (_Float16).
using half = sycl::half;

};  // namespace nvtype

#endif  // __DTYPE_HPP__
```

### common/launch.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/launch.cuh
// SYCL port of the launch macros + atomics.
//   cuda_linear_index       -> it.get_global_linear_id() (handled in linear_launch)
//   cuda_linear_launch(k,s,n,...) -> linear_launch(q, n, [=](size_t idx){...})
//   atomicAdd / atomicCAS   -> atomic_fetch_add / atomic_cas below
// v1 uses seq_cst for all hash-table atomics (correctness first; relax in step 5).
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __LAUNCH_HPP__
#define __LAUNCH_HPP__

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace nv {

constexpr int LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS = 512;

inline size_t divup(size_t a, size_t b) { return (a + b - 1) / b; }

// ---- atomic helpers (device-side; operate on global USM pointers) ----
// Memory order: seq_cst by default (correctness-first v1). Compile with
// -DVOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS to switch to relaxed — a safe optimization because
// insert (build_hash_table) and lookup (voxelization) live in SEPARATE kernels
// serialized by the in-order queue + intervening D2H memcpy, so there is no
// cross-kernel insert/lookup race. Within each kernel the CAS / fetch_add only
// need atomicity (relaxed), not ordering: the CAS-winner uniquely owns the
// value-slot store, and num_points_per_voxel fetch_add returns distinct slots.
// Cross-kernel visibility of hash-table writes is guaranteed by queue ordering,
// independent of intra-kernel memory_order.
#ifdef VOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS
inline constexpr sycl::memory_order kAtomicOrder = sycl::memory_order::relaxed;
#else
inline constexpr sycl::memory_order kAtomicOrder = sycl::memory_order::seq_cst;
#endif

template <typename T>
inline T atomic_fetch_add(T* addr, T operand) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<T, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  return a.fetch_add(operand);
}

// CUDA atomicCAS(addr, compare, val) returns the OLD value.
// sycl atomic_ref::compare_exchange_strong(expected, desired) returns bool and,
// on failure, writes the actual value into `expected`. So returning `expected`
// after the call yields the old value in both success and failure cases.
inline std::uint32_t atomic_cas(std::uint32_t* addr, std::uint32_t compare, std::uint32_t val) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  std::uint32_t expected = compare;
  a.compare_exchange_strong(expected, val);
  return expected;
}

inline std::uint32_t atomic_load(std::uint32_t* addr) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  return a.load();
}

inline void atomic_store(std::uint32_t* addr, std::uint32_t val) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  a.store(val);
}

// ---- launch helper ----
// Equivalent of: cuda_linear_launch(kernel, stream, num, ...) which launches
// divup(num,512) blocks of 512 threads; the kernel receives the global 1D index
// and does its own bounds check (if (idx >= num) return;).
//
// Usage:
//   linear_launch(queue, num_points, [=](size_t idx){
//       build_hash_table_kernel(idx, num_points, points, ...);
//   });
// Returns the sycl::event for the submitted kernel so callers can use SYCL
// event profiling (requires the queue to be built with enable_profiling).
template <typename Kernel>
sycl::event linear_launch(sycl::queue& queue, size_t num, Kernel kernel) {
  if (num == 0) return sycl::event{};
  size_t blocks = divup(num, static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS));
  sycl::range<1> global{blocks * static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS)};
  sycl::range<1> local{static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS)};
  return queue.submit([&](sycl::handler& h) {
    h.parallel_for(sycl::nd_range<1>{global, local},
                   [=](sycl::nd_item<1> it) {
                     size_t idx = it.get_global_linear_id();
                     kernel(idx);
                   });
  });
}

};  // namespace nv

#endif  // __LAUNCH_HPP__
```

### lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/bevfusion/lidar-voxelization.cu
// SYCL port. Algorithm preserved 1:1 (hash table + scatter + reduce_mean, half, XYZ/ZYX).
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#include "lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp"
#include "common/launch.hpp"
#include "common/check.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace bevfusion {
namespace lidar {

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// device-side helpers (compiled for device when called from parallel_for)
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

static inline uint64_t hash(uint64_t k) {
  k ^= k >> 16;
  k *= 0x85ebca6b;
  k ^= k >> 13;
  k *= 0xc2b2ae35;
  k ^= k >> 16;
  return k;
}

static inline void insert_to_hash_table(const uint32_t key, uint32_t* value, const uint32_t hash_size,
                                        uint32_t* hash_table) {
  uint64_t hash_value = hash(key);
  uint32_t slot = hash_value % (hash_size / 2);  // key, value
  uint32_t empty_key = UINT32_MAX;
  while (true) {
    // CUDA: pre_key = atomicCAS(hash_table + slot, empty_key, key);
    uint32_t pre_key = nv::atomic_cas(hash_table + slot, empty_key, key);
    if (pre_key == empty_key) {
      uint32_t vid = nv::atomic_fetch_add<uint32_t>(value, 1u);
      nv::atomic_store(hash_table + slot + hash_size / 2, vid);
      break;
    } else if (pre_key == key) {
      break;
    }
    slot = (slot + 1) % (hash_size / 2);
  }
}

static inline uint32_t lookup_hash_table(const uint32_t key, const uint32_t hash_size, uint32_t* hash_table) {
  uint64_t hash_value = hash(key);
  uint32_t slot = hash_value % (hash_size / 2);  // key, value
  uint32_t empty_key = UINT32_MAX;
  while (true /* need to be adjusted according to data*/) {
    uint32_t k = nv::atomic_load(hash_table + slot);
    if (k == key) {
      return nv::atomic_load(hash_table + slot + hash_size / 2);
    } else if (k == empty_key) {
      return empty_key;
    } else {
      slot = (slot + 1) % (hash_size / 2);
    }
  }
  return empty_key;
}

template <CoordinateOrder order>
static inline void save_result_by_order(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y, unsigned int z);

template <>
inline void save_result_by_order<CoordinateOrder::XYZ>(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y,
                                                       unsigned int z) {
  *output = sycl::uint4{0, x, y, z};
}

template <>
inline void save_result_by_order<CoordinateOrder::ZYX>(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y,
                                                       unsigned int z) {
  *output = sycl::uint4{0, z, y, x};
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// kernels (each takes the global 1D index; bounds-checks itself)
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

static inline void build_hash_table_kernel(size_t point_idx, size_t points_size, const sycl::half* points,
                                           VoxelizationParameter param, uint32_t* hash_table,
                                           uint32_t* real_voxel_num) {
  if (point_idx >= points_size) return;

  float px = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 0];
  float py = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 1];
  float pz = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 2];

  int voxel_idx = (int)sycl::floor((px - param.min_range.x) / param.voxel_size.x);
  if (voxel_idx < 0 || voxel_idx >= param.grid_size.x) return;

  int voxel_idy = (int)sycl::floor((py - param.min_range.y) / param.voxel_size.y);
  if (voxel_idy < 0 || voxel_idy >= param.grid_size.y) return;

  int voxel_idz = (int)sycl::floor((pz - param.min_range.z) / param.voxel_size.z);
  if (voxel_idz < 0 || voxel_idz >= param.grid_size.z) return;

  uint32_t voxel_offset = (voxel_idz * param.grid_size.y + voxel_idy) * param.grid_size.x + voxel_idx;
  insert_to_hash_table(voxel_offset, real_voxel_num, (uint32_t)(points_size * 2 * 2), hash_table);
}

template <CoordinateOrder order>
static inline void voxelization_kernel(size_t point_idx, size_t points_size, const sycl::half* points,
                                       VoxelizationParameter param, uint32_t* hash_table,
                                       uint32_t* num_points_per_voxel, float* voxels_temp,
                                       uint32_t* voxel_indices) {
  if (point_idx >= points_size) return;

  float px = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 0];
  float py = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 1];
  float pz = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 2];

  if (px < param.min_range.x || px >= param.max_range.x || py < param.min_range.y || py >= param.max_range.y ||
      pz < param.min_range.z || pz >= param.max_range.z) {
    return;
  }

  int voxel_idx = (int)sycl::floor((px - param.min_range.x) / param.voxel_size.x);
  int voxel_idy = (int)sycl::floor((py - param.min_range.y) / param.voxel_size.y);
  int voxel_idz = (int)sycl::floor((pz - param.min_range.z) / param.voxel_size.z);
  if (voxel_idx < 0 || voxel_idx >= param.grid_size.x) return;
  if (voxel_idy < 0 || voxel_idy >= param.grid_size.y) return;
  if (voxel_idz < 0 || voxel_idz >= param.grid_size.z) return;

  uint32_t voxel_offset = (voxel_idz * param.grid_size.y + voxel_idy) * param.grid_size.x + voxel_idx;

  uint32_t voxel_id = lookup_hash_table(voxel_offset, (uint32_t)(points_size * 2 * 2), hash_table);
  if (voxel_id >= (uint32_t)param.max_voxels) {
    return;
  }

  uint32_t current_num = nv::atomic_fetch_add<uint32_t>(num_points_per_voxel + voxel_id, 1u);
  if (current_num < (uint32_t)param.max_points_per_voxel) {
    uint32_t dst_offset =
        voxel_id * (param.num_feature * param.max_points_per_voxel) + current_num * param.num_feature;
    uint32_t src_offset = (uint32_t)point_idx * param.num_feature;
    for (int feature_idx = 0; feature_idx < param.num_feature; ++feature_idx) {
      voxels_temp[dst_offset + feature_idx] = (float)points[src_offset + feature_idx];
    }
    // Multiple points in the same voxel write the identical (x,y,z) — idempotent.
    save_result_by_order<order>((sycl::uint4*)voxel_indices + voxel_id, (unsigned int)voxel_idx,
                                (unsigned int)voxel_idy, (unsigned int)voxel_idz);
  }
}

static inline void reduce_mean_kernel(size_t voxel_idx, size_t num_voxels, float* voxels_temp,
                                      uint32_t* num_points_per_voxel, int max_points_per_voxel, int feature_num,
                                      sycl::half* voxel_features) {
  if (voxel_idx >= num_voxels) return;

  num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx] = num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx] > (uint32_t)max_points_per_voxel
                                        ? (uint32_t)max_points_per_voxel
                                        : num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx];
  int valid_points_num = (int)num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx];
  // opt2: reduce_mean is launched over max_voxels (not the host-known real count)
  // to avoid a mid-pipeline host sync, so slots beyond the real voxel count have
  // num_points == 0 (memset) and voxels_temp == 0xff — skip them (would div-by-0).
  if (valid_points_num <= 0) return;
  int offset = voxel_idx * max_points_per_voxel * feature_num;
  // opt4: accumulate in registers with a single sequential pass over the valid
  // points (point-major layout → consecutive addresses), then one write of the
  // mean per feature. The original loop did a global read-modify-write on
  // voxels_temp[offset+f] every (feature,point) iteration; this avoids those
  // intermediate global writes and reads the point data sequentially.
  // (feature_num is small for lidar — BEVFusion/CenterPoint use 5; cap 16.)
  float acc[16] = {0.f};
  const int feat = feature_num;
  for (int p = 0; p < valid_points_num; ++p) {
    const float* pt = voxels_temp + offset + p * feat;
    for (int f = 0; f < feat; ++f) acc[f] += pt[f];
  }
  sycl::half* out = voxel_features + voxel_idx * feat;
  const float inv = 1.0f / (float)valid_points_num;
  for (int f = 0; f < feat; ++f) out[f] = (sycl::half)(acc[f] * inv);
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// VoxelizationParameter::compute_grid_size
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

nvtype::Int3 VoxelizationParameter::compute_grid_size(const nvtype::Float3& max_range, const nvtype::Float3& min_range,
                                                      const nvtype::Float3& voxel_size) {
  nvtype::Int3 size;
  size.x = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.x - min_range.x) / voxel_size.x));
  size.y = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.y - min_range.y) / voxel_size.y));
  size.z = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.z - min_range.z) / voxel_size.z));
  return size;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// VoxelizationImplement
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

// Build the internal GPU queue. Profiling is opt-in via the VOX_PROFILE env
// var: when set, enable_profiling is added so last_profile() can report per-
// kernel device time. When unset the queue is plain (no profiling overhead) —
// this keeps the B-path bench binary fair when profiling isn't requested.
static sycl::queue make_internal_queue() {
  sycl::device dev{sycl::gpu_selector_v};
  if (std::getenv("VOX_PROFILE")) {
    return sycl::queue{dev, sycl::property_list{sycl::property::queue::in_order{},
                                                 sycl::property::queue::enable_profiling{}}};
  }
  return sycl::queue{dev, sycl::property::queue::in_order{}};
}

class VoxelizationImplement : public Voxelization {
 public:
  VoxelizationImplement() {}
  virtual ~VoxelizationImplement() { free_all(); }

  bool init(VoxelizationParameter param) {
    this->param_ = param;
    this->profiling_enabled_ = (std::getenv("VOX_PROFILE") != nullptr);
    this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.x, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.z + 1};

    this->hash_table_size_ = param_.max_points * 2 * 2 * sizeof(unsigned int);
    this->voxels_temp_size_ = param_.max_voxels * param_.max_points_per_voxel * param_.num_feature * sizeof(float);
    this->voxel_features_size_ =
        param_.max_voxels * param_.max_points_per_voxel * param_.num_feature * sizeof(sycl::half);
    this->voxel_num_size_ = param_.max_voxels * sizeof(unsigned int);
    this->voxel_idxs_size_ = param_.max_voxels * 4 * sizeof(unsigned int);

    hash_table_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(hash_table_size_, queue_);
    voxels_temp_ = (float*)sycl::malloc_device(voxels_temp_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_features_ = (sycl::half*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_features_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_num_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_num_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_indices_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_idxs_size_, queue_);
    d_real_num_voxels_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(sizeof(unsigned int), queue_);
    h_real_num_voxels_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_host(sizeof(unsigned int), queue_);
    if (h_real_num_voxels_) *h_real_num_voxels_ = 0;  // sane before first forward

    if (!hash_table_ || !voxels_temp_ || !d_voxel_features_ || !d_voxel_num_ || !d_voxel_indices_ ||
        !d_real_num_voxels_ || !h_real_num_voxels_) {
      std::cerr << "[VoxelizationImplement] device/host allocation failed\n";
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  void free_all() {
    auto rel = [&](void* p) {
      if (p) sycl::free(p, queue_);
    };
    rel(hash_table_);
    rel(voxels_temp_);
    rel(d_voxel_features_);
    rel(d_voxel_num_);
    rel(d_voxel_indices_);
    rel(d_real_num_voxels_);
    rel(h_real_num_voxels_);
    hash_table_ = nullptr;
    voxels_temp_ = nullptr;
    d_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_num_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_indices_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_features_ = nullptr;
    h_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  }

  // points and voxels must be of half type
  virtual void forward(const nvtype::half* points, int num_points, void* stream,
                       CoordinateOrder output_order) override {
    sycl::queue& q = stream ? *static_cast<sycl::queue*>(stream) : queue_;
    const sycl::half* _points = reinterpret_cast<const sycl::half*>(points);

    q.memset(hash_table_, 0xff, hash_table_size_);
    // opt3: dropped the voxels_temp_ 0xff memset (32MB). reduce_mean only reads
    // the [0, valid) point slots of valid voxels, and every one of those slots is
    // written by voxelization this frame (the first max_pts points have
    // current_num < max_pts and always write). Stale inter-frame data in unread
    // slots is never observed. hash_table 0xff (empty_key) and the two 0 memsets
    // (counters) are still required.
    q.memset(d_voxel_num_, 0, voxel_num_size_);
    q.memset(d_real_num_voxels_, 0, sizeof(unsigned int));

    size_t points_size = (size_t)num_points;

    // SYCL device lambdas cannot implicitly capture `this`; copy members by value.
    auto param = param_;
    uint32_t* hash_table = hash_table_;
    uint32_t* d_real_num_voxels = d_real_num_voxels_;
    uint32_t* d_voxel_num = d_voxel_num_;
    float* voxels_temp = voxels_temp_;
    uint32_t* d_voxel_indices = d_voxel_indices_;
    sycl::half* d_voxel_features = d_voxel_features_;

    ev_build_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
      build_hash_table_kernel(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_real_num_voxels);
    });

    // D2H of real voxel count (async, ordered after build_hash_table in in-order queue)
    q.memcpy(h_real_num_voxels_, d_real_num_voxels_, sizeof(int));

    if (output_order == CoordinateOrder::XYZ) {
      ev_vox_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
        voxelization_kernel<CoordinateOrder::XYZ>(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_voxel_num,
                                                   voxels_temp, d_voxel_indices);
      });
      this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.x, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.z + 1};
    } else if (output_order == CoordinateOrder::ZYX) {
      ev_vox_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
        voxelization_kernel<CoordinateOrder::ZYX>(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_voxel_num,
                                                   voxels_temp, d_voxel_indices);
      });
      this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.z + 1, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.x};
    } else {
      Assertf(false, "Invalid output_order: %d", static_cast<int>(output_order));
    }

    // opt2: NO mid-pipeline host sync. The D2H memcpy of the voxel count was
    // submitted above (in-order, async) and is NOT waited on here — previously a
    // q.wait()+host read forced a host round-trip just to size the reduce_mean
    // launch, which profiling showed was the largest wall-time component after
    // opt1. Instead launch reduce_mean over max_voxels and let the kernel
    // self-guard (valid_points_num<=0 → return) on empty slots. The host reads
    // the count lazily in num_voxels() after the caller's final wait.
    int max_points_per_voxel = param_.max_points_per_voxel;
    int num_feature = param_.num_feature;
    int max_voxels = param_.max_voxels;
    ev_reduce_ = nv::linear_launch(q, (size_t)max_voxels, [=](size_t idx) {
      reduce_mean_kernel(idx, (size_t)max_voxels, voxels_temp, d_voxel_num, max_points_per_voxel, num_feature,
                         d_voxel_features);
    });
  }

  virtual void* internal_stream() override { return &queue_; }

  virtual bool last_profile(uint64_t& build_hash_ns, uint64_t& voxelization_ns,
                            uint64_t& reduce_mean_ns) override {
    if (!profiling_enabled_) return false;
    auto dur_ns = [](const sycl::event& e) -> uint64_t {
      // A default-constructed / empty event (e.g. linear_launch with num==0)
      // has no profiling info — get_profiling_info throws, so guard it.
      try {
        uint64_t s = e.get_profiling_info<sycl::info::event_profiling::command_start>();
        uint64_t en = e.get_profiling_info<sycl::info::event_profiling::command_end>();
        return en - s;
      } catch (...) {
        return 0;
      }
    };
    build_hash_ns = dur_ns(ev_build_);
    voxelization_ns = dur_ns(ev_vox_);
    reduce_mean_ns = dur_ns(ev_reduce_);
    return true;
  }

  virtual unsigned int num_voxels() override {
    // opt2: forward() no longer blocks to read the count; read it lazily from the
    // host-pinned buffer here. Valid only after the queue is idle (caller's wait).
    real_num_voxels_ = *h_real_num_voxels_;
    return real_num_voxels_;
  }
  virtual unsigned int voxel_dim() override { return param_.num_feature; }
  virtual unsigned int indices_dim() override { return 4; }
  virtual std::vector<int> grid_size() override { return output_grid_size_; }
  virtual const void* indices() override { return d_voxel_indices_; }
  virtual const void* features() override { return d_voxel_features_; }
  virtual CoordinateOrder order() override { return order_; }

 private:
  sycl::queue queue_{make_internal_queue()};

  CoordinateOrder order_ = CoordinateOrder::NoneOrder;
  VoxelizationParameter param_;
  unsigned int real_num_voxels_ = 0;
  std::vector<int> output_grid_size_;
  bool profiling_enabled_ = false;

  // Profiling events from the most recent forward() (valid only if profiling_enabled_).
  sycl::event ev_build_;
  sycl::event ev_vox_;
  sycl::event ev_reduce_;

  uint32_t* hash_table_ = nullptr;
  float* voxels_temp_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* h_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_voxel_num_ = nullptr;
  sycl::half* d_voxel_features_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_voxel_indices_ = nullptr;
  size_t hash_table_size_;
  size_t voxels_temp_size_;
  size_t voxel_features_size_;
  size_t voxel_idxs_size_;
  size_t voxel_num_size_;
};

std::shared_ptr<Voxelization> create_voxelization(VoxelizationParameter param) {
  std::shared_ptr<VoxelizationImplement> impl(new VoxelizationImplement());
  if (!impl->init(param)) {
    impl.reset();
  }
  return impl;
}

};  // namespace lidar
};  // namespace bevfusion
```

### lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/bevfusion/lidar-voxelization.hpp
// SYCL port of the voxelization interface. Algorithm/semantics unchanged.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__
#define __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "common/dtype.hpp"

namespace bevfusion {
namespace lidar {

enum class CoordinateOrder : int {
  NoneOrder = 0,
  XYZ = 1,  // BEVFusion
  ZYX = 2   // CenterPoint
};

struct VoxelizationParameter {
  nvtype::Float3 min_range;
  nvtype::Float3 max_range;
  nvtype::Float3 voxel_size;
  nvtype::Int3 grid_size;
  int num_feature;
  int max_voxels;
  int max_points_per_voxel;
  int max_points;

  static nvtype::Int3 compute_grid_size(const nvtype::Float3& max_range, const nvtype::Float3& min_range,
                                        const nvtype::Float3& voxel_size);
};

class Voxelization {
 public:
  virtual ~Voxelization() = default;
  // points and voxels must be of half-float device pointer.
  // stream is a sycl::queue* (nullptr => use the implementation's internal queue).
  virtual void forward(const nvtype::half* points, int num_points, void* stream = nullptr,
                       CoordinateOrder output_order = CoordinateOrder::XYZ) = 0;

  virtual unsigned int num_voxels() = 0;
  virtual unsigned int voxel_dim() = 0;
  virtual unsigned int indices_dim() = 0;
  virtual std::vector<int> grid_size() = 0;
  virtual const void* indices() = 0;
  virtual const void* features() = 0;
  virtual CoordinateOrder order() = 0;

  // Returns the implementation's internal sycl::queue* so callers can allocate
  // USM in the SAME context (cast the void* to sycl::queue*).
  virtual void* internal_stream() = 0;

  // Per-kernel device profiling from the most recent forward() call.
  // Returns true if profiling was enabled (queue built with enable_profiling,
  // i.e. VOX_PROFILE env var was set at construction) and a run has completed;
  // the three out-params are filled with each kernel's device execution time in
  // nanoseconds. Returns false otherwise (out-params untouched). The caller must
  // ensure the queue is idle (e.g. q.wait()) before calling.
  virtual bool last_profile(uint64_t& build_hash_ns, uint64_t& voxelization_ns,
                            uint64_t& reduce_mean_ns) = 0;
};

std::shared_ptr<Voxelization> create_voxelization(VoxelizationParameter parameter);

};  // namespace lidar
};  // namespace bevfusion

#endif  // __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__
```

### scripts/ssh_ab.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Controlled A/B: build opt3 and opt4 relaxed profile binaries, alternate runs in one session.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
# opt3 snapshot voxelizer.cpp (local) — opt4 is already on intel as the current voxelizer.cpp
OPT3_LOCAL = '/tmp/migration_snapshots/opt3_nomemset_2026-06-25/lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp'
fout = open('/tmp/ab_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
c = pexpect.spawn('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***', timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

# transfer opt3 voxelizer.cpp to /tmp/vox_opt3.cpp on intel
data = open(OPT3_LOCAL, 'rb').read()
local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
    c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
    c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
c.sendline('base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > /tmp/vox_opt3.cpp; echo MD5V=$(md5sum /tmp/vox_opt3.cpp|cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
log(f'[local] opt3 voxelizer.cpp local_md5={local_md5}\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
# opt4 = current voxelizer.cpp (already on intel). copy both to /tmp.
cp lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp /tmp/vox_opt4.cpp
COMMON=(-fsycl -std=c++17 -O2 -g -I . -ffp-model=precise -fno-fast-math -fimf-precision=high -DVOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS)
echo "===== compile opt3 + opt4 profile binaries ====="
icpx "${COMMON[@]}" /tmp/vox_opt3.cpp lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_profile.cpp -o /tmp/prof_o3rel 2>&1 | tail -3
echo "O3_RC=$?"
icpx "${COMMON[@]}" /tmp/vox_opt4.cpp lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_profile.cpp -o /tmp/prof_o4rel 2>&1 | tail -3
echo "O4_RC=$?"
ls -l /tmp/prof_o3rel /tmp/prof_o4rel

B=291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831
echo "===== ALTERNATING A/B on $B (3 rounds each, 20 runs/round) ====="
for round in 1 2 3; do
  echo "----- round $round OPT3 -----"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu VOX_PROFILE=1 /tmp/prof_o3rel lidar_voxelization/test/$B.bin 20 XYZ 2>&1 | grep -E 'build_hash_table|voxelization |reduce_mean|kernel sum|wall '
  echo "----- round $round OPT4 -----"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu VOX_PROFILE=1 /tmp/prof_o4rel lidar_voxelization/test/$B.bin 20 XYZ 2>&1 | grep -E 'build_hash_table|voxelization |reduce_mean|kernel sum|wall '
done
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZAB_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZAB_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=900)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
log('\n[local] done\n')
fout.close()
```

### scripts/ssh_apull.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# A path: check jump docker mirrors, pull CUDA devel image (background), report.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/apull_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
log('[local] on jump (A path)\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
echo "===== docker daemon.json mirrors ====="
cat /etc/docker/daemon.json 2>/dev/null
echo "===== docker info registries ====="
docker info 2>/dev/null | grep -iE 'registry|mirror|proxy' 
echo "===== test reach nvidia/cuda via daocloud mirror ====="
docker pull docker.m.daocloud.io/nvidia/cuda:12.2.0-devel-ubuntu20.04 2>&1 | tail -25
echo "PULL_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
echo "===== verify image ====="
docker images 2>&1 | grep -iE 'cuda|REPOSITORY'
echo "===== nvidia-smi in container quick test ====="
docker run --rm --gpus all docker.m.daocloud.io/nvidia/cuda:12.2.0-devel-ubuntu20.04 nvidia-smi -L 2>&1 | head -5
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZA_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZA_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=900)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] A-pull done\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_apull3.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# A path retry: pull CUDA image with --progress=plain, log to file on jump, poll.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, time
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/apull3_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
log('[local] on jump — A pull retry (plain progress, logged)\n')
# Run pull with plain progress, log to /tmp/cuda_pull.log, run detached so it survives.
inner = r'''
set +e
nohup docker pull docker.m.daocloud.io/nvidia/cuda:12.2.0-devel-ubuntu20.04 --quiet > /tmp/cuda_pull.log 2>&1 &
PULLPID=$!
echo "PULL_PID=$PULLPID"
# wait up to ~1100s, polling log
for i in $(seq 1 110); do
  if ! kill -0 $PULLPID 2>/dev/null; then echo "PULL_FINISHED after ${i}0s"; break; fi
  sleep 10
done
echo "===== pull log tail ====="
tail -15 /tmp/cuda_pull.log 2>&1
echo "PULL_EXIT_CODE=$(wait $PULLPID 2>/dev/null; echo $?)"
echo "===== images ====="
docker images 2>&1 | grep -iE 'cuda|REPOSITORY' | head -5
echo "===== quick GPU test ====="
docker run --rm --gpus all docker.m.daocloud.io/nvidia/cuda:12.2.0-devel-ubuntu20.04 bash -c 'nvcc --version | tail -1; nvidia-smi -L' 2>&1 | head -5
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZA3_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZA3_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=1200)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] A-pull3 done\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_benchbuild.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# B1: transfer dense_ref + harness + build.sh, mkdir, rebuild, run bench_compare.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
FILES = [
    ('build.sh', 'build.sh'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.hpp', 'lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.hpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.cpp', 'lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.cpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/common/voxelizer_utils.hpp', 'lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/common/voxelizer_utils.hpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test/test_bench_compare.cpp', 'lidar_voxelization/test/test_bench_compare.cpp'),
]
fout = open('/tmp/benchbuild_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel\n')

# mkdir dense_ref dirs
c.sendline(f'mkdir -p {DEST}/lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars {DEST}/lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/common; echo MKD_DONE')
c.expect('MKD_DONE', timeout=15)

for rel, target in FILES:
    data = open(os.path.join(MIG, rel), 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
    c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
    c.sendline(f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{target}; echo DECODE=$?; '
               f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{target} | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); dec = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); rm = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
    log(f'[local] {target}: {"OK" if (dec=="0" and rm==local_md5) else "BAD"}\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "===== BUILD ====="
bash build.sh 2>&1 | tail -12
echo "BUILD_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
ls -l test_bench_compare 2>&1
for b in synth_50000 synth_100000 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831; do
  echo "===== RUN bench_compare $b ====="
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_bench_compare lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 15 2>&1
  echo "RC=$?"
done
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZBB_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZBB_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=600)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done\n')
```

```
import pexpect, sys, re, base64

cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'

  echo "=====END $f====="
```

### scripts/ssh_catconn.py
```
import pexpect, sys, re
cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=60, encoding='utf-8')
c.logfile_read = sys.stdout
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline('***REDACTED***')
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
def run(cmd, marker='ZZC_2207'):
    c.sendline(cmd + f' ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker)+r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=60)
run('echo "=====connect_***REDACTED***.sh====="; cat ~/connect_***REDACTED***.sh 2>/dev/null || cat ./connect_***REDACTED***.sh 2>/dev/null || find ~ -maxdepth 2 -name connect_***REDACTED***.sh 2>/dev/null')
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
```

```
echo "--- find main.cpp / CMakeLists referencing voxel ---"
find / -maxdepth 7 -name "CMakeLists.txt" 2>/dev/null | xargs grep -l -i voxel 2>/dev/null | grep -v proc | head
' 2>&1 | head -90
```

### scripts/ssh_diag.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Diagnose: where is icpx / sycl compiler in the container, what is PATH after setvars.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/diag_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

inner = r'''
set +e
source /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh > /tmp/sv.log 2>&1
echo "SETVARS_EXIT=$?"
echo "===== which icpx / icx / clang++ ====="
which icpx icx clang++ clang 2>&1
echo "===== compiler bin dir ====="
ls /opt/intel/oneapi/compiler/latest/bin/ 2>&1 | head -40
echo "===== PATH ====="
echo "$PATH" | tr ':' '\n'
echo "===== find icpx anywhere ====="
find /opt/intel -name icpx -type f 2>/dev/null | head
echo "===== sycl-ls ====="
which sycl-ls 2>&1
sycl-ls 2>&1 | head -10
echo "===== END DIAG ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
docker_cmd = (
    'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
    '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
    '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
    f"-c 'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash'"
)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZDIAG_5512_EXIT=$?')
c.expect(re.escape('ZDIAG_5512_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=300)
c.logfile_read = None
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
print("done", os.path.getsize('/tmp/diag_dump.txt'))
```

### scripts/ssh_diag2.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Find the real SYCL compiler in the container.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/diag2_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

inner = r'''
set +e
source /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh > /tmp/sv.log 2>&1
echo "===== compiler 2025.3 bin full listing ====="
ls -la /opt/intel/oneapi/compiler/2025.3/bin/ 2>&1
echo "===== latest symlink? ====="
ls -la /opt/intel/oneapi/compiler/latest 2>&1
echo "===== search icpx / icx / clang++ / sycl-ls anywhere ====="
find / -name 'icpx' 2>/dev/null | head
find / -name 'icx' 2>/dev/null | head
find / -name 'clang++' 2>/dev/null | head
find / -name 'sycl-ls' 2>/dev/null | head
echo "===== how was /home/tfcc/bevfusion built? ====="
ls -la /home/tfcc/bevfusion/ 2>&1
ls -la /home/tfcc/bevfusion/build/ 2>&1 | head -30
echo "--- CMakeCache compiler lines ---"
grep -iE 'CMAKE_(CXX|C)_COMPILER|FSYCL|SYCL' /home/tfcc/bevfusion/build/CMakeCache.txt 2>/dev/null | head -20
echo "--- any build.sh / compile script in bevfusion ---"
find /home/tfcc/bevfusion -maxdepth 2 -name '*.sh' 2>/dev/null
echo "===== how were vector-add built? check for compile script in /work ====="
ls -la /work/bevfusion_migration/*.sh 2>&1
grep -riE 'icpx|icx|clang|fsycl' /work/bevfusion_migration/*.cpp /work/bevfusion_migration/*.sh 2>/dev/null | head
echo "===== file type of an existing SYCL exe ====="
file /work/bevfusion_migration/vector-add-usm 2>&1
echo "===== END DIAG2 ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
docker_cmd = (
    'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
    '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
    '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
    f"-c 'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash'"
)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZDIAG2_6610_EXIT=$?')
c.expect(re.escape('ZDIAG2_6610_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=300)
c.logfile_read = None
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
print("done", os.path.getsize('/tmp/diag2_dump.txt'))
```

### scripts/ssh_diag3.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Reveal the REAL compile command used by the bevfusion build, and find any c++ compiler.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/diag3_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

inner = r'''
set +e
echo "===== bevfusion build.sh ====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/build.sh
echo "===== /usr/bin/c++ identity ====="
readlink -f /usr/bin/c++
/usr/bin/c++ --version 2>&1 | head -3
echo "===== the weird compiler subdir ====="
ls -la /opt/intel/oneapi/compiler/2025.3/bin/compiler/ 2>&1
echo "===== any clang* / dpcpp / icpx anywhere (full) ====="
find / \( -name 'clang*' -o -name 'dpcpp' -o -name 'icpx' -o -name 'icx' \) -type f 2>/dev/null | head -30
echo "===== compile_commands.json: first 2 entries (the real compile invocation) ====="
python3 -c "import json;d=json.load(open('/home/tfcc/bevfusion/build/compile_commands.json'));[print('CMD['+str(i)+']:',e['command'][:400],'\n  FILE:',e['file'],'\n') for i,e in enumerate(d[:3])]" 2>&1
echo "===== is bevfusion src actually SYCL? ====="
grep -rl 'sycl' /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src 2>/dev/null | head
echo "--- a voxelizer src head ---"
find /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src -name '*voxel*' 2>/dev/null
echo "===== g++ -fsycl support? ====="
echo 'int main(){return 0;}' > /tmp/t.cpp
g++ -fsycl /tmp/t.cpp -o /tmp/t 2>&1 | head -5
echo "g++ -fsycl exit=$?"
echo "===== END DIAG3 ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
docker_cmd = (
    'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
    '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
    '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
    f"-c 'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash'"
)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZDIAG3_7720_EXIT=$?')
c.expect(re.escape('ZDIAG3_7720_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=300)
c.logfile_read = None
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
print("done", os.path.getsize('/tmp/diag3_dump.txt'))
```

### scripts/ssh_diag4.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Check the Intel HOST (outside docker) for an icpx compiler and available docker images.
import pexpect, sys, re, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/diag4_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

# Run directly ON THE HOST (no docker), capture output.
def hostrun(script, marker, timeout=120):
    c.logfile_read = fout
    c.sendline(script + f' ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    c.logfile_read = None

hostrun(r'''
echo "===== HOST: which icpx / icx / clang++ / sycl-ls ====="
which icpx icx clang++ sycl-ls 2>&1
echo "===== HOST: /opt/intel ====="
ls /opt/intel 2>&1
ls /opt/intel/oneapi 2>&1 | head
echo "===== HOST: find icpx on host ====="
find /opt/intel /usr -name icpx 2>/dev/null | head
echo "===== HOST: icpx --version if setvars ====="
if [ -f /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh ]; then
  set +u; source /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh >/tmp/sv.log 2>&1; set -u
  which icpx; icpx --version 2>&1 | head -3
fi
echo "===== HOST: docker images ====="
docker images 2>&1 | head -30
echo "===== HOST: docker ps -a ====="
docker ps -a 2>&1 | head -20
echo "===== END DIAG4 ====="
''', 'ZDIAG4')

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
print("done", os.path.getsize('/tmp/diag4_dump.txt'))
```

### scripts/ssh_fetch.py
```
import pexpect, sys, re, base64

def connect():
    cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
    c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=60, encoding='utf-8')
    i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
    if i == 1:
        c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
    c.sendline('***REDACTED***')
    c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
    return c

def run(c, cmd, timeout=600, marker='ZZFETCH_7741'):
    c.sendline(cmd + f' ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    return c.match.group(1)

c = connect()
c.sendline('echo CONN_OK'); c.expect('CONN_OK', timeout=15)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_OK'); c.expect('INTEL_OK', timeout=20)

inner = r'''
echo "=====TREE src====="
find /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src -type f | sort
echo "=====GREP cvops====="
grep -rilE 'resize|remap|cvtcolor|nonmax|nms|normalize|rotate' /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: README.md====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/README.md 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: CMakeLists.txt====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/CMakeLists.txt 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: build.sh====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/build.sh 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: voxelizer_sycl.hpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src/bevfusion_unified/voxelizer_sycl.hpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: voxelizer_sycl.cpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src/bevfusion_unified/voxelizer_sycl.cpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: pp_voxelizer.hpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src/pointpillars/voxelizer.hpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: pp_voxelizer.cpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src/pointpillars/voxelizer.cpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: voxelizer_utils.hpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/src/common/voxelizer_utils.hpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====FILE: test_pointpillars_voxelizer.cpp====="
cat /home/tfcc/bevfusion/test/test_pointpillars_voxelizer.cpp 2>/dev/null
echo "=====END ALL====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()

# log to file only
fout = open('/tmp/container_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
c.logfile_read = fout

run(c, f'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN -e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu --entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion -c \'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash\'',
    timeout=600)

fout.close()
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)

# report sizes
import os
sz = os.path.getsize('/tmp/container_dump.txt')
print(f"DUMP_SIZE={sz} bytes")
```

### scripts/ssh_findnvidia.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Search jump host + reachable hosts for NVIDIA GPU (P2200?).
import pexpect, sys, re, base64
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/findnvidia_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
log('[local] on jump host\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
echo "===== JUMP hostname ====="
hostname; uname -a
echo "===== JUMP nvidia-smi ====="
which nvidia-smi 2>&1; nvidia-smi -L 2>&1 | head
echo "===== JUMP lspci VGA/3D ====="
lspci 2>/dev/null | grep -iE 'vga|3d|nvidia' 
echo "===== JUMP nvcc ====="
which nvcc 2>&1
echo "===== known hosts / ssh config (other machines?) ====="
cat ~/.ssh/known_hosts 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}' | head -20
cat ~/.ssh/config 2>/dev/null | head -30
echo "===== connect_***REDACTED***.sh content (what other hosts it knows) ====="
cat ~/connect_***REDACTED***.sh 2>/dev/null || find / -name "connect_***REDACTED***.sh" 2>/dev/null | head
echo "===== ls home for scripts hinting at other machines ====="
ls -la ~/ 2>/dev/null | head -30
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZN_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZN_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=120)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_fixbuild.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Re-transfer the 3 fixed files, compile on HOST, run on host (opencl backend),
# fall back to container run if host run fails.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
FILES = [
    ('lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp',      'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp',      'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test_voxelizer.cpp', 'lidar_voxelization/test_voxelizer.cpp'),
]
fout = open('/tmp/fixbuild_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel host\n')

# --- transfer 3 files ---
for rel, target in FILES:
    data = open(os.path.join(MIG, rel), 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
    c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
    c.sendline(f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{target}; echo DECODE=$?; '
               f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{target} | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); dec = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); rm = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
    log(f'[local] {target}: {"OK" if (dec=="0" and rm==local_md5) else "BAD"} ({local_md5}/{rm})\n')

# --- compile + run script (runs ON THE HOST) ---
inner = r'''
set +e
echo "===== HOST COMPILE ====="
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
bash build.sh 2>&1 | tail -30
echo "BUILD_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
ls -l test_voxelizer 2>&1
if [ ! -x test_voxelizer ]; then echo "NO_BINARY"; echo "===== END ====="; exit 0; fi

echo "===== HOST RUN (opencl backend) ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer 2>&1
echo "HOST_RUN_RC=$?"

echo "===== END HOSTBUILD ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZHB_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZHB_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=500)
c.logfile_read = None

# --- if host run failed (segfault/nonzero), try container run ---
# Check the dump for HOST_RUN_RC
fout.flush()
dump = open('/tmp/fixbuild_dump.txt').read()
need_container = ('HOST_RUN_RC=0' not in dump) or ('ALL TESTS PASSED' not in dump)
if need_container and 'NO_BINARY' not in dump:
    log('\n[local] host run did not pass -> trying CONTAINER run (opencl backend)\n')
    cinner = r'''
set +e
source /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh > /tmp/sv.log 2>&1
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "===== CONTAINER RUN (opencl backend) ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer 2>&1
echo "CONTAINER_RUN_RC=$?"
echo "===== END CONTAINER ====="
'''
    cb64 = base64.b64encode(cinner.encode()).decode()
    docker_cmd = (
        'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
        '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
        '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
        f"-c 'echo {cb64} | base64 -d | bash'"
    )
    c.logfile_read = fout
    c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZCR_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape('ZCR_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=500)
    c.logfile_read = None

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done, dump size {os.path.getsize("/tmp/fixbuild_dump.txt")}\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_hostbuild.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Compile ON THE HOST (icpx is in PATH via ~/.bashrc) and try running on the host.
import pexpect, sys, re, os, base64
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/hostbuild_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)

def hostrun(script, marker, timeout=400):
    b64 = base64.b64encode(script.encode()).decode()
    c.logfile_read = fout
    c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?")
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    c.logfile_read = None

hostrun(r'''
set +e
echo "===== icpx --version ====="
icpx --version 2>&1 | head -4
echo "===== sycl-ls (host GPU visibility) ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu sycl-ls 2>&1 | head -20
echo "===== BUILD on host ====="
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
bash build.sh 2>&1
echo "BUILD_RC=$?"
ls -l test_voxelizer 2>&1
echo "===== RUN on host ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer 2>&1
echo "RUN_RC=$?"
echo "===== END HOSTBUILD ====="
''', 'ZHB')

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
print("done", os.path.getsize('/tmp/hostbuild_dump.txt'))
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Check jump host network connectivity (no pull). Is it intranet-only?
import pexpect, sys, re, base64
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/jumpnet_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
log('[local] on jump (net check only)\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
echo "===== /etc/docker/daemon.json ====="
cat /etc/docker/daemon.json 2>/dev/null || echo "(no daemon.json or no read)"
echo "===== docker info Registry Mirrors ====="
docker info 2>/dev/null | grep -iA3 -E 'Registry Mirrors|Insecure Registries'
echo "===== env proxy ====="
env | grep -iE 'proxy' 
echo "===== ping/curl tests (3s timeout each) ====="
echo "-- dockerhub --"; curl -sS -m 3 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/ 2>&1
echo "-- daocloud mirror --"; curl -sS -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://docker.m.daocloud.io/v2/ 2>&1
echo "-- desaysv --"; curl -sS -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://docker.desaysv.com/v2/ 2>&1
echo "-- generic internet (baidu) --"; curl -sS -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://www.baidu.com 2>&1
echo "===== existing images registries ====="
docker images 2>&1 | awk '{print $1}' | sort -u
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZN_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZN_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=120)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
```

### scripts/ssh_neighb.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Transfer updated build.sh + test_voxelizer_real.cpp, rebuild, run boundary-shift analysis.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
FILES = [
    ('build.sh', 'build.sh'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_real.cpp', 'lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_real.cpp'),
]
fout = open('/tmp/neighb_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel host\n')

for rel, target in FILES:
    data = open(os.path.join(MIG, rel), 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
    c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
    c.sendline(f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{target}; echo DECODE=$?; '
               f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{target} | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); dec = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); rm = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
    log(f'[local] {target}: {"OK" if (dec=="0" and rm==local_md5) else "BAD"} ({local_md5}/{rm})\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "===== BUILD ====="
bash build.sh 2>&1 | tail -4
echo "BUILD_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b; do
  echo "===== RUN real test $b ====="
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer_real lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 2>&1
  echo "RC=$?"
done
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZN_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZN_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=600)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done, dump {os.path.getsize("/tmp/neighb_dump.txt")}B\n')
```
