### scripts/ssh_probe.py
```
import pexpect, sys

cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
child = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=60, encoding='utf-8')
child.logfile_read = sys.stdout

i = child.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    child.sendline('yes')
    child.expect('password:')
child.sendline('***REDACTED***')

# Wait for a shell prompt
child.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
child.sendline('echo ===MARKER_CONNECTED===')
child.expect('===MARKER_CONNECTED===', timeout=20)
print("\n[CONNECTED]\n")

def run(c, timeout=90):
    child.sendline(c + ' ; echo ===DONE_EXIT=$?===')
    child.expect('===DONE_EXIT=([0-9]+)===', timeout=timeout)
    return child.match.group(1)

# 1. Run the connect script the user asked for
print("\n##### ./connect_***REDACTED***.sh #####")
run('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh', timeout=120)

# 2. Gather environment for the porting analysis
print("\n##### WHOAMI / PWD #####")
run('whoami; pwd')
print("\n##### OS #####")
run('cat /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null | head -5; uname -a')
print("\n##### GPU/ACCELERATOR (lspci) #####")
run('lspci 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "vga|display|3d|intel|nvidia" || echo no_lspci')
print("\n##### NVIDIA #####")
run('nvidia-smi 2>/dev/null | head -20 || echo no_nvidia_smi')
print("\n##### Intel oneAPI / SYCL / DPCPP #####")
run('which icpx icx dpcpp syclcc 2>/dev/null; echo "---"; ls /opt/intel/oneapi 2>/dev/null || echo no_oneapi')
print("\n##### Intel XPU (sycl-ls) #####")
run('sycl-ls 2>/dev/null || echo no_sycl_ls')
print("\n##### xpu-smi #####")
run('xpu-smi discovery 2>/dev/null | head -30 || echo no_xpu_smi')
print("\n##### Python / torch /框架 #####")
run('python3 --version 2>/dev/null; pip3 list 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "torch|intel|onnx|tensorrt|cuda|sycl|dpctl|oneccl" || echo no_pip_pkgs')
print("\n##### connect_***REDACTED***.sh 内容 #####")
run('cat ./connect_***REDACTED***.sh 2>/dev/null || echo no_script')
print("\n##### HOME / 工作目录内容 #####")
run('ls -la ~ 2>/dev/null | head -40')

child.sendline('exit')
child.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
```

### scripts/ssh_probe3.py
```
import pexpect, sys, re, base64

def connect():
    cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
    c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=60, encoding='utf-8')
    c.logfile_read = sys.stdout
    i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
    if i == 1:
        c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
    c.sendline('***REDACTED***')
    c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
    return c

def run(c, cmd, timeout=90, marker='ZZENDPROBE_9281'):
    c.sendline(cmd + f' ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    return c.match.group(1)

c = connect()
c.sendline('echo CONN_OK'); c.expect('CONN_OK', timeout=15)
print("\n[JUMP HOST OK]\n")

print("\n===== 进入 Intel 机 =====")
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_OK'); c.expect('INTEL_OK', timeout=20)
print("\n[INTEL HOST OK]\n")

print("\n--- docker 是否安装 / 镜像列表 ---")
run(c, 'which docker; docker images 2>/dev/null', timeout=60)

print("\n--- 目标镜像 inspect (Env/Cmd/Entrypoint) ---")
run(c, 'docker image inspect intel/tfcc:bevfusion 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "Cmd|Entrypoint|Env|WorkingDir" -A1 | head -60', timeout=60)

# Build read-only inspection script, base64 to avoid quoting issues
inner = r'''
set +e
echo "### whoami/host/os"
whoami; hostname; cat /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null | head -4
echo "### compilers"
which icpx icx dpcpp clang++ 2>/dev/null
icpx --version 2>/dev/null | head -2
echo "### oneapi root"
ls /opt/intel/oneapi 2>/dev/null
ls /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh 2>/dev/null || echo NO_SETVARS
echo "### sycl-ls"
sycl-ls 2>/dev/null || echo NO_SYCL_LS
echo "### xpu-smi"
xpu-smi discovery 2>/dev/null | head -20 || echo NO_XPU_SMI
echo "### python"
python3 --version 2>/dev/null
echo "### pip pkgs (torch/intel/ov/onnx/sycl)"
python3 -m pip list 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "torch|intel-extension|openvino|onnx|dpctl|sycl|onec|cuda|tensorrt|numba" || echo none
echo "### torch + xpu"
python3 -c "import torch;print('torch',torch.__version__);print('xpu_avail',torch.xpu.is_available());print('xpu_cnt',torch.xpu.device_count())" 2>/dev/null || echo NO_TORCH
echo "### ipex"
python3 -c "import intel_extension_for_pytorch as ipex;print('ipex',ipex.__version__)" 2>/dev/null || echo NO_IPEX
echo "### openvino"
python3 -c "import openvino;print('ov',openvino.__version__)" 2>/dev/null || echo NO_OV
echo "### dpctl"
python3 -c "import dpctl;print('dpctl',dpctl.__version__)" 2>/dev/null || echo NO_DPCTL
echo "### bevfusion/voxel 相关文件"
find / -path /proc -prune -o -path /sys -prune -o -path /dev -prune -o \( -iname '*bevfusion*' -o -iname '*voxel*' \) -print 2>/dev/null | head -30
echo "### 工作目录"
ls -la /workspace 2>/dev/null | head -20; echo "---"; ls -la /work 2>/dev/null | head -20; echo "---"; ls /opt 2>/dev/null
echo "### 容器内关键 env"
env | grep -iE 'ONEAPI|SYCL|LD_LIBRARY|PYTHONPATH|^PATH=' | head -20
echo "### END_INNER"
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()

print("\n--- 进容器只读探查 (docker run --rm, 不挂载/不写) ---")
run(c, f'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN -e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu intel/tfcc:bevfusion bash -c \'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash\'',
    timeout=420, marker='ZZENDPROBE_9281')

c.sendline('exit')
c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit')
c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
print("\n[ALL DONE]\n")
```

### scripts/ssh_probe4.py
```
import pexpect, sys, re, base64

def connect():
    cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
    c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=60, encoding='utf-8')
    c.logfile_read = sys.stdout
    i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
    if i == 1:
        c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
    c.sendline('***REDACTED***')
    c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
    return c

def run(c, cmd, timeout=420, marker='ZZENDPROBE_9281'):
    c.sendline(cmd + f' ; echo {marker}_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_EXIT=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    return c.match.group(1)

c = connect()
c.sendline('echo CONN_OK'); c.expect('CONN_OK', timeout=15)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_OK'); c.expect('INTEL_OK', timeout=20)
print("\n[INTEL HOST OK]\n")

inner = r'''
set +e
echo "### whoami/host/os"
whoami; hostname; cat /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null | head -4
echo "### compilers"
which icpx icx dpcpp clang++ 2>/dev/null
icpx --version 2>/dev/null | head -2
echo "### oneapi root"
ls /opt/intel/oneapi 2>/dev/null
ls /opt/intel/oneapi/setvars.sh 2>/dev/null || echo NO_SETVARS
echo "### sycl-ls"
sycl-ls 2>&1 | head -20 || echo NO_SYCL_LS
echo "### xpu-smi"
xpu-smi discovery 2>&1 | head -20 || echo NO_XPU_SMI
echo "### python"
python3 --version 2>/dev/null
echo "### pip pkgs"
python3 -m pip list 2>/dev/null | grep -iE "torch|intel-extension|openvino|onnx|dpctl|sycl|onec|cuda|tensorrt|numba" || echo none
echo "### torch + xpu"
python3 -c "import torch;print('torch',torch.__version__);print('xpu_avail',torch.xpu.is_available());print('xpu_cnt',torch.xpu.device_count())" 2>&1 | head -10 || echo NO_TORCH
echo "### ipex"
python3 -c "import intel_extension_for_pytorch as ipex;print('ipex',ipex.__version__)" 2>&1 | head -5 || echo NO_IPEX
echo "### openvino"
python3 -c "import openvino;print('ov',openvino.__version__)" 2>&1 | head -5 || echo NO_OV
echo "### dpctl"
python3 -c "import dpctl;print('dpctl',dpctl.__version__)" 2>&1 | head -5 || echo NO_DPCTL
echo "### /home/tfcc 内容"
ls -la /home/tfcc 2>/dev/null | head -30
echo "### bevfusion 目录"
ls -la /home/tfcc/bevfusion 2>/dev/null | head -40
echo "### bevfusion/voxel 文件搜索"
find /home -path /proc -prune -o \( -iname '*bevfusion*' -o -iname '*voxel*' \) -print 2>/dev/null | head -40
echo "### 工作目录"
ls -la /workspace 2>/dev/null | head -20; echo "--- /work ---"; ls -la /work 2>/dev/null | head -20
echo "### 容器内 env"
env | grep -iE 'ONEAPI|SYCL|LD_LIBRARY|PYTHONPATH|^PATH=' | head -20
echo "### END_INNER"
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()

print("\n--- 进容器只读探查 (修正 entrypoint) ---")
run(c, f'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN -e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu --entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion -c \'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash\'',
    timeout=480)

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
print("\n[ALL DONE]\n")

### scripts/ssh_profile.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# C-path step 1: transfer modified sources + new profile test, build, run per-kernel profiling.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'; DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
FILES = [
    'common/launch.hpp',
    'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp',
    'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp',
    'lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_profile.cpp',
    'build.sh',
]
fout = open('/tmp/profile_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] connected to intel\n')

def transfer(rel):
    data = open(os.path.join(MIG, rel), 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
    c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
    c.sendline(f'mkdir -p $(dirname {DEST}/{rel}); base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{rel}; echo DECODE=$?; echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{rel} | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
    log(f'[local] {rel}: local_md5={local_md5}\n')

for rel in FILES:
    transfer(rel)

# verify md5s explicitly with one command
c.sendline(f'cd {DEST} && for f in {" ".join(FILES)}; do echo $(md5sum "$f" | cut -d" " -f1) $f; done; echo MD5DONE')
c.expect('MD5DONE', timeout=30)
log('[local] md5 list above\n')

inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "===== BUILD ====="
bash build.sh 2>&1 | tail -12
echo "BUILD_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
ls -l test_voxelizer_profile 2>&1
for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b synth_50000 synth_100000 synth_500000 synth_1000000; do
  echo "===== PROFILE $b (XYZ) ====="
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu VOX_PROFILE=1 ./test_voxelizer_profile lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 20 XYZ 2>&1
  echo "RC=$?"
done
echo "===== END ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZPROF_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZPROF_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=900)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log('\n[local] done\n')
```

for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b; do
  echo "--- real $b ---"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer_real lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 2>&1 | tail -6
done
echo "===== CORRECTNESS relaxed (opt1) ====="
for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b; do
  echo "--- real_relaxed $b ---"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer_real_relaxed lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 2>&1 | tail -6
done

echo "===== PROFILE seq_cst (baseline) ====="
for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b synth_50000 synth_100000; do
  echo "--- profile $b ---"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu VOX_PROFILE=1 ./test_voxelizer_profile lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 20 XYZ 2>&1 | grep -E 'loaded|voxels produced|build_hash|voxelization|reduce_mean|kernel sum|wall|overhead|throughput'
done
echo "===== PROFILE relaxed (opt1) ====="
for b in 291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831 3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b synth_50000 synth_100000; do
  echo "--- profile_relaxed $b ---"
  ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu VOX_PROFILE=1 ./test_voxelizer_profile_relaxed lidar_voxelization/test/$b.bin 20 XYZ 2>&1 | grep -E 'loaded|voxels produced|build_hash|voxelization|reduce_mean|kernel sum|wall|overhead|throughput'
done
echo "===== END ====="
'''

fout.close()
```

### scripts/ssh_realbuild.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Part 2: transfer reorganized code (build.sh, test/test_voxelizer.cpp,
# test/test_voxelizer_real.cpp), rm old test_voxelizer.cpp location, compile, run.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
FILES = [
    ('build.sh',                                    'build.sh'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer.cpp',      'lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer.cpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_real.cpp', 'lidar_voxelization/test/test_voxelizer_real.cpp'),
]
fout = open('/tmp/realbuild_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel host\n')

# rm old-location test_voxelizer.cpp (moved into test/)
c.sendline(f'rm -f {DEST}/lidar_voxelization/test_voxelizer.cpp; echo RMOLD_DONE')
c.expect('RMOLD_DONE', timeout=15)
log('[local] removed old test_voxelizer.cpp location\n')

# transfer code files
for rel, target in FILES:
    data = open(os.path.join(MIG, rel), 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
    c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
    c.sendline(f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{target}; echo DECODE=$?; '
               f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{target} | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); dec = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); rm = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
    log(f'[local] {target}: {"OK" if (dec=="0" and rm==local_md5) else "BAD"} ({local_md5}/{rm})\n')

# compile + run
inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "===== LS test/ ====="
ls -la lidar_voxelization/test/
echo "===== BUILD ====="
bash build.sh 2>&1 | tail -25
echo "BUILD_RC=${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
ls -l test_voxelizer test_voxelizer_real 2>&1
echo "===== RUN small test ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer 2>&1
echo "SMALL_RC=$?"
echo "===== RUN real test (.bin 1) ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer_real lidar_voxelization/test/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin 2>&1
echo "REAL1_RC=$?"
echo "===== RUN real test (.bin 2) ====="
ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu ./test_voxelizer_real lidar_voxelization/test/3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b.bin 2>&1
echo "REAL2_RC=$?"
echo "===== END ALL ====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(inner.encode()).decode()
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(f"echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash ; echo ZRB_EXIT=$?")
c.expect(re.escape('ZRB_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=600)
c.logfile_read = None
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done, dump {os.path.getsize("/tmp/realbuild_dump.txt")}B\n')
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Re-transfer just build.sh (fixed set -u issue) and rebuild+run in container.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os

JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'
fout = open('/tmp/rebuild_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel host\n')

# --- transfer build.sh only ---
data = open(os.path.join(MIG, 'build.sh'), 'rb').read()
local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]
c.sendline('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo RMK'); c.expect('RMK', timeout=10)
for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
    c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
    c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=20)
c.sendline(f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/build.sh; echo DECODE=$?; '
           f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/build.sh | cut -d" " -f1); rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; echo ENDFILE')
c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=20); dec = c.match.group(1)
c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20); rm = c.match.group(1)
c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)
log(f'[local] build.sh: local={local_md5} remote={rm} decode={dec} '
    f'{"OK" if (dec=="0" and rm==local_md5) else "BAD"}\n')

# --- build + run ---
build_inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "=====BUILD====="
bash build.sh
echo "BUILD_EXIT=$?"
echo "=====RUN====="
./test_voxelizer
echo "RUN_EXIT=$?"
echo "=====ALL_DONE====="
'''
b64 = base64.b64encode(build_inner.encode()).decode()
docker_cmd = (
    'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
    '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
    '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
    f"-c 'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash'"
)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZBR_7733_EXIT=$?')
c.expect(re.escape('ZBR_7733_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=600)
c.logfile_read = None

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()

### scripts/ssh_scp_bins.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Step 1: transfer the two real .bin point clouds to intel:/work/bevfusion_migration/data/
#   local --(scp password)--> jump --(scp -o BindAddress=***REDACTED***, key)--> intel
import pexpect, sys, re, hashlib, os

LOCAL_BINS = [
  '/home/V01/uid05044/uid05044/code/Lidar_AI_Solution-master/CUDA-CenterPoint/data/test/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin',
  '/home/V01/uid05044/uid05044/code/Lidar_AI_Solution-master/CUDA-CenterPoint/data/test/3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b.bin',
]
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/scp_transfer_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

local_md5 = {os.path.basename(p): hashlib.md5(open(p,'rb').read()).hexdigest() for p in LOCAL_BINS}
log(f'[local] local md5:\n'); [log(f'  {k}: {v}\n') for k,v in local_md5.items()]

# ---------- Phase A: scp local -> jump (password) ----------
log('\n[local] Phase A: scp local -> jump\n')
files = ' '.join(f"'{p}'" for p in LOCAL_BINS)
scp_cmd = (f'scp -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
           f'{files} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:mig_data/')
c = pexpect.spawn(scp_cmd, timeout=300, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=300)
c.logfile_read = None
log('[local] Phase A done\n')

# ---------- Phase B: ssh jump, then scp -> intel ----------
log('\n[local] Phase B: ssh jump -> scp -> intel\n')
cmd = 'ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***'
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=120, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline('mkdir -p ~/mig_data; ls -la ~/mig_data/; echo LSA_DONE')
c.expect('LSA_DONE', timeout=20)

# mkdir data on intel via one-shot ssh
c.sendline("ssh -b ***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** 'mkdir -p /work/bevfusion_migration/data && echo MKDIR_OK'; echo MK_RC=$?")
c.expect('MKDIR_OK', timeout=60)
c.expect('MK_RC=(\d+)', timeout=20); log(f'[local] intel mkdir exit={c.match.group(1)}\n')

# scp each file jump -> intel
for p in LOCAL_BINS:
    name = os.path.basename(p)
    c.sendline(f"scp -o BindAddress=***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no "
               f"-o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ~/mig_data/{name} "
               f"***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:/work/bevfusion_migration/data/ ; echo SCPRC_{name}=$?")
    c.expect(re.escape(f'SCPRC_{name}=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=180)
    log(f'[local] scp {name} -> intel exit={c.match.group(1)}\n')

# verify on intel: ls + md5 via one-shot ssh
c.sendline("ssh -b ***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** "
           "'ls -la /work/bevfusion_migration/data/; "
           "for f in /work/bevfusion_migration/data/*.bin; do md5sum $f; done; echo VERIFY_DONE'")
c.expect('VERIFY_DONE', timeout=60)
log('\n[local] verify output captured above\n')

c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done, dump {os.path.getsize("/tmp/scp_transfer_dump.txt")}B\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_scp_bins2.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Step 1 (fixed): transfer two real .bin point clouds to intel:/work/bevfusion_migration/data/
#   1) ssh jump: mkdir ~/mig_data          (password)
#   2) scp local -> jump:~/mig_data/       (password, dir now exists)
#   3) ssh jump -> scp -> intel (BindAddress) + md5 verify
import pexpect, sys, re, hashlib, os

LOCAL_BINS = [
  '/home/V01/uid05044/uid05044/code/Lidar_AI_Solution-master/CUDA-CenterPoint/data/test/291e7331922541cea98122b607d24831.bin',
  '/home/V01/uid05044/uid05044/code/Lidar_AI_Solution-master/CUDA-CenterPoint/data/test/3615d82e7e8546fea5f181157f42e30b.bin',
]
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/scp_transfer_dump2.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

local_md5 = {os.path.basename(p): hashlib.md5(open(p,'rb').read()).hexdigest() for p in LOCAL_BINS}
log('[local] local md5:\n'); [log(f'  {k}: {v}\n') for k,v in local_md5.items()]

SSH_OPTS = '-o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null'

def ssh_jump_oneshot(inline_cmd, timeout=60):
    """Run a single command on jump via one-shot ssh (password)."""
    c = pexpect.spawn(f'ssh {SSH_OPTS} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** {repr(inline_cmd)}',
                      timeout=timeout, encoding='utf-8')
    i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
    if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
    c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
    c.logfile_read = fout
    c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=timeout)
    c.logfile_read = None

# ---- 1) mkdir mig_data on jump ----
log('\n[local] 1) mkdir mig_data on jump\n')
ssh_jump_oneshot('mkdir -p ~/mig_data && echo MKJUMP_OK')

# ---- 2) scp local -> jump:~/mig_data/ ----
log('\n[local] 2) scp local -> jump\n')
files = ' '.join(f"'{p}'" for p in LOCAL_BINS)
c = pexpect.spawn(f'scp {SSH_OPTS} {files} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:mig_data/',
                  timeout=300, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=300)
c.logfile_read = None
log('[local] scp to jump done\n')

# ---- 3) ssh jump, scp -> intel, verify ----
log('\n[local] 3) ssh jump -> scp -> intel + verify\n')
c = pexpect.spawn(f'ssh {SSH_OPTS} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***', timeout=120, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.logfile_read = fout
c.sendline('ls -la ~/mig_data/; echo LSJUMP_DONE')
c.expect('LSJUMP_DONE', timeout=20)

# mkdir target test/ dir on intel via one-shot ssh
c.sendline("ssh -b ***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** "
           "'mkdir -p /work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test && echo MKDIR_OK'; echo MK_RC=$?")
c.expect('MKDIR_OK', timeout=60)
c.expect('MK_RC=(\d+)', timeout=20); log(f'[local] intel mkdir exit={c.match.group(1)}\n')

# scp each file jump -> intel
for p in LOCAL_BINS:
    name = os.path.basename(p)
    c.sendline(f"scp -o BindAddress=***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no "
               f"-o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ~/mig_data/{name} "
               f"***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:/work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test/ ; echo SCPRC_{name}=$?")
    c.expect(re.escape(f'SCPRC_{name}=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=300)
    log(f'[local] scp {name} -> intel exit={c.match.group(1)}\n')

# verify on intel
c.sendline("ssh -b ***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** "
           "'ls -la /work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test/; "
           "md5sum /work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test/*.bin; echo VERIFY_DONE'")
c.expect('VERIFY_DONE', timeout=60)
log('\n[local] verify captured above\n')
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done, dump {os.path.getsize("/tmp/scp_transfer_dump2.txt")}B\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_scp_synth.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Transfer 4 synthetic .bin to intel lidar_voxelization/test/ via jump.
import pexpect, sys, re, hashlib, os
LOCAL_BINS = [f'/tmp/migration_src/lidar_voxelization/test/synth_{N}.bin' for N in [50000,100000,500000,1000000]]
JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
fout = open('/tmp/scp_synth_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')
def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush(); sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()
local_md5 = {os.path.basename(p): hashlib.md5(open(p,'rb').read()).hexdigest() for p in LOCAL_BINS}
log('[local] md5:\n'); [log(f'  {k}: {v}\n') for k,v in local_md5.items()]
SSH_OPTS = '-o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null'

def ssh_jump_oneshot(inline_cmd, timeout=60):
    c = pexpect.spawn(f'ssh {SSH_OPTS} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** {repr(inline_cmd)}', timeout=timeout, encoding='utf-8')
    i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
    if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
    c.sendline(JUMP_PW); c.logfile_read = fout; c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=timeout); c.logfile_read=None

log('\n[local] 1) mkdir mig_data on jump\n')
ssh_jump_oneshot('mkdir -p ~/mig_data && echo MKJUMP_OK')

log('\n[local] 2) scp local -> jump\n')
files = ' '.join(f"'{p}'" for p in LOCAL_BINS)
c = pexpect.spawn(f'scp {SSH_OPTS} {files} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:mig_data/', timeout=300, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW); c.logfile_read=fout; c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=300); c.logfile_read=None
log('[local] scp to jump done\n')

log('\n[local] 3) jump -> intel + verify\n')
c = pexpect.spawn(f'ssh {SSH_OPTS} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***', timeout=120, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1: c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW); c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30); c.logfile_read=fout
for p in LOCAL_BINS:
    name = os.path.basename(p)
    c.sendline(f"scp -o BindAddress=***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null "
               f"~/mig_data/{name} ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***:/work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test/ ; echo SCPRC_{name}=$?")
    c.expect(re.escape(f'SCPRC_{name}=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=300)
    log(f'[local] scp {name} -> intel exit={c.match.group(1)}\n')
c.sendline("ssh -b ***REDACTED*** -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** "
           "'cd /work/bevfusion_migration/lidar_voxelization/test && md5sum synth_*.bin && echo VERIFY_DONE'")
c.expect('VERIFY_DONE', timeout=60)
log('\n[local] verify captured\n')
try:
    c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
except Exception: pass
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] done\n')
```

### scripts/ssh_transfer_build.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Transfer the 7 ported SYCL files from local /tmp/migration_src/ to the Intel
# host's /work/bevfusion_migration/ via the existing pexpect SSH tunnel
# (local -> jump ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED*** -> ./connect_***REDACTED***.sh -> ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***).
# scp jump->intel failed (exit=1), so we stream base64 in <1500-char chunks
# through the PTY (well under the ~4096 line limit) and verify with md5sum.
import pexpect, sys, re, base64, hashlib, os

JUMP_PW = '***REDACTED***'
MIG = '/tmp/migration_src'
DEST = '/work/bevfusion_migration'

# (local_path, relative_target)
FILES = [
    ('build.sh',                                'build.sh'),
    ('common/dtype.hpp',                        'common/dtype.hpp'),
    ('common/check.hpp',                        'common/check.hpp'),
    ('common/launch.hpp',                       'common/launch.hpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp',        'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp',        'lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp'),
    ('lidar_voxelization/test_voxelizer.cpp',   'lidar_voxelization/test_voxelizer.cpp'),
]

fout = open('/tmp/transfer_dump.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8')

def log(s):
    fout.write(s); fout.flush()
    sys.stdout.write(s); sys.stdout.flush()

# ---- connect to jump ----
cmd = ('ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null '
       '-o ConnectTimeout=15 ***REDACTED***@***REDACTED***')
c = pexpect.spawn(cmd, timeout=90, encoding='utf-8')
i = c.expect(['password:', 'yes/no', pexpect.EOF, pexpect.TIMEOUT])
if i == 1:
    c.sendline('yes'); c.expect('password:')
c.sendline(JUMP_PW)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
log('[local] on jump host\n')

# ---- go to intel host ----
c.sendline('./connect_***REDACTED***.sh')
c.expect(['***REDACTED***', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=60)
c.expect([r'\$', r'#', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=30)
c.sendline('echo INTEL_READY'); c.expect('INTEL_READY', timeout=20)
log('[local] on intel host\n')

# helper: run a command on intel shell, wait for MARKER
def run(cmd, marker, timeout=60):
    c.sendline(cmd + f' ; echo {marker}_DONE=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape(marker) + r'_DONE=(\d+)', timeout=timeout)
    return c.match.group(1)

# ---- verify /work writable ----
rc = run(f'touch {DEST}/.xfer_test && rm {DEST}/.xfer_test', 'WRK', timeout=15)
log(f'[local] /work writable check exit={rc}\n')
if rc != '0':
    log('[local] FATAL: /work not writable\n')
    fout.close(); sys.exit(1)

# ---- transfer each file ----
results = []
for rel, target in FILES:
    local = os.path.join(MIG, rel)
    data = open(local, 'rb').read()
    local_md5 = hashlib.md5(data).hexdigest()
    b64 = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
    chunks = [b64[i:i+1500] for i in range(0, len(b64), 1500)]

    # ensure target dir
    tgtdir = os.path.dirname(target)
    run(f'mkdir -p {DEST}/{tgtdir}', 'MK', timeout=15)
    run('rm -f /tmp/mig.b64', 'RM', timeout=10)

    for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks):
        # base64 alphabet has no shell-special chars; safe unquoted as one arg
        c.sendline(f'printf %s {ch} >> /tmp/mig.b64; echo CK{idx}')
        c.expect(re.escape(f'CK{idx}') + r'\r?\n', timeout=30)

    # decode to target, capture remote md5 with a clear prefix
    c.sendline(
        f'base64 -d /tmp/mig.b64 > {DEST}/{target} 2>/tmp/dec.err; '
        f'echo DECODE=$?; rm -f /tmp/mig.b64; '
        f'echo MD5V=$(md5sum {DEST}/{target} | cut -d" " -f1); echo ENDFILE'
    )
    c.expect('DECODE=(\d+)', timeout=30)
    dec_rc = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('MD5V=([0-9a-f]{32})', timeout=20)
    remote_md5 = c.match.group(1)
    c.expect('ENDFILE', timeout=15)

    ok = (dec_rc == '0' and remote_md5 == local_md5)
    results.append((target, ok, local_md5, remote_md5))
    log(f'[local] {target}: local={local_md5} remote={remote_md5} '
        f'decode={dec_rc} {"OK" if ok else "MISMATCH"}\n')

# ---- summary ----
allok = all(r[1] for r in results)
log('\n[local] === TRANSFER SUMMARY ===\n')
for t, ok, lm, rm in results:
    log(f'  {"OK " if ok else "BAD"} {t}\n')
log(f'[local] all files transferred correctly: {allok}\n')

# ---- list dest tree ----
c.sendline(f'find {DEST} -type f -printf "%p %s\\n" | sort; echo LISTER_DONE')
c.expect('LISTER_DONE', timeout=30)

# ---- if all OK, build + run inside container (writable mount, no :ro) ----
if allok:
    build_inner = r'''
set +e
cd /work/bevfusion_migration
echo "=====LS====="
ls -la
ls -la common lidar_voxelization
echo "=====BUILD====="
bash build.sh
echo "BUILD_EXIT=$?"
echo "=====RUN====="
./test_voxelizer
echo "RUN_EXIT=$?"
echo "=====ALL_DONE====="
'''
    b64 = base64.b64encode(build_inner.encode()).decode()
    docker_cmd = (
        'docker run --rm --device /dev/dri --group-add 992 --cap-add=SYS_ADMIN '
        '-e ONEAPI_DEVICE_SELECTOR=opencl:gpu -v /work/:/work/ '
        '--entrypoint /bin/bash intel/tfcc:bevfusion '
        f"-c 'echo {b64} | base64 -d | bash'"
    )
    c.logfile_read = fout
    c.sendline(docker_cmd + ' ; echo ZBR_7733_EXIT=$?')
    c.expect(re.escape('ZBR_7733_EXIT=') + r'(\d+)', timeout=600)
    c.logfile_read = None

# ---- exit cleanly ----
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect([r'\$', pexpect.TIMEOUT], timeout=15)
c.sendline('exit'); c.expect(pexpect.EOF, timeout=10)
fout.close()
log(f'\n[local] dump size: {os.path.getsize("/tmp/transfer_dump.txt")}\n')
```

### lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/common/voxelizer_utils.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace bevfusion::voxelizer {

inline int div_up(int value, int divisor)
{
    return value / divisor + ((value % divisor) > 0);
}

inline int atomic_fetch_add(int* addr, int operand)
{
    sycl::atomic_ref<int,
                     sycl::memory_order::relaxed,
                     sycl::memory_scope::device,
                     sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
        obj(*addr);
    return obj.fetch_add(operand);
}

inline std::uint32_t next_power(std::uint32_t value)
{
    value--;
    value |= value >> 1;
    value |= value >> 2;
    value |= value >> 4;
    value |= value >> 8;
    value |= value >> 16;
    value++;
    return value;
}

}  // namespace bevfusion::voxelizer
```

### lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.cpp
```
// Copyright (C) 2018-2026 Intel Corporation
// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0

#include "pointpillars/voxelizer.hpp"
#include "common/voxelizer_utils.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace pointpillars {

namespace {

// Kernel 1: scatter each point into the cell's per-pillar buffer.
//   pillar_count[cell]         — running count of points in the cell
//   pillar_buffer[cell][n][c]  — raw (x, y, z, i) copied in order of arrival
// Points beyond max_num_points_per_voxel are dropped (matches mmdet3d).
void ScatterPointsKernel(const float* pts, int num_points, int point_dim,
                         int grid_x, int grid_y, int grid_z,
                         float min_x, float min_y, float min_z,
                         float vx, float vy, float vz,
                         int max_pts_per_voxel,
                         int* pillar_count, float* pillar_buffer,
                         sycl::nd_item<3> item) {
    const int idx = item.get_local_id(2)
                  + item.get_group(2) * item.get_local_range().get(2);
    if (idx >= num_points) return;

    const float px = pts[idx * point_dim + 0];
    const float py = pts[idx * point_dim + 1];
    const float pz = pts[idx * point_dim + 2];

    const int xi = static_cast<int>(sycl::floor((px - min_x) / vx));
    const int yi = static_cast<int>(sycl::floor((py - min_y) / vy));
    const int zi = static_cast<int>(sycl::floor((pz - min_z) / vz));

    if (xi < 0 || xi >= grid_x ||
        yi < 0 || yi >= grid_y ||
        zi < 0 || zi >= grid_z) return;

    // cell index — order doesn't matter for correctness as long as it's
    // consistent with the compact kernel below.
    const int cell = (zi * grid_y + yi) * grid_x + xi;

    // Reserve a slot in the dense pillar buffer.
    const int slot = bevfusion::voxelizer::atomic_fetch_add(&pillar_count[cell], 1);
    if (slot >= max_pts_per_voxel) return;

    // Copy raw point (x, y, z, intensity) into pillar_buffer[cell][slot]
    float* dst = pillar_buffer + (static_cast<size_t>(cell) * max_pts_per_voxel + slot) * point_dim;
    dst[0] = px;
    dst[1] = py;
    dst[2] = pz;
    if (point_dim >= 4) {
        dst[3] = pts[idx * point_dim + 3];
    }
    for (int k = 4; k < point_dim; ++k) {
        dst[k] = pts[idx * point_dim + k];
    }
}

// Kernel 2: compact active cells into a sparse voxel list.
//   coors[slot] = (0, x_idx, y_idx, z_idx)   ← NOTE: (batch, x, y, z)
//                                              matching mmdet3d Voxelization
//                                              + F.pad((1,0), value=batch).
//                                              See bev_0422_dev.md §29.4.
//   num_voxels[slot] = min(count, max_pts_per_voxel)
//   features[slot]   = pillar_buffer[cell]   (copied as-is; pad zero)
void CompactPillarsKernel(const int* pillar_count, const float* pillar_buffer,
                          int grid_x, int grid_y,
                          int max_voxels, int max_pts_per_voxel, int point_dim,
                          int* counter,
                          float* features, int* num_voxels, int* coors,
                          int x_idx, int y_idx, int z_idx) {
    const int cell = (z_idx * grid_y + y_idx) * grid_x + x_idx;
    const int raw_count = pillar_count[cell];
    if (raw_count == 0) return;

    const int slot = bevfusion::voxelizer::atomic_fetch_add(counter, 1);
    if (slot >= max_voxels) return;

    const int clamped = (raw_count < max_pts_per_voxel) ? raw_count : max_pts_per_voxel;

    // coors layout (batch=0, x_idx, y_idx, z_idx)
    coors[slot * 4 + 0] = 0;
    coors[slot * 4 + 1] = x_idx;
    coors[slot * 4 + 2] = y_idx;
    coors[slot * 4 + 3] = z_idx;

    num_voxels[slot] = clamped;

    const float* src = pillar_buffer
                     + (static_cast<size_t>(cell) * max_pts_per_voxel) * point_dim;
    float* dst = features
               + (static_cast<size_t>(slot) * max_pts_per_voxel) * point_dim;
    // Copy the used pillar points; padding region stays zeroed from memset.
    for (int n = 0; n < clamped; ++n) {
        const float* s = src + n * point_dim;
        float* d = dst + n * point_dim;
        for (int c = 0; c < point_dim; ++c) {
            d[c] = s[c];
        }
    }
}

}  // namespace

Voxelizer::Voxelizer(sycl::queue& queue, const VoxelizerConfig& config)
    : queue_(queue), config_(config) {
    const float range_x = config_.pc_range[3] - config_.pc_range[0];
    const float range_y = config_.pc_range[4] - config_.pc_range[1];
    const float range_z = config_.pc_range[5] - config_.pc_range[2];

    grid_x_ = static_cast<int>(std::round(range_x / config_.voxel_size[0]));
    grid_y_ = static_cast<int>(std::round(range_y / config_.voxel_size[1]));
    grid_z_ = static_cast<int>(std::round(range_z / config_.voxel_size[2]));

    if (grid_x_ <= 0 || grid_y_ <= 0 || grid_z_ <= 0) {
        throw std::runtime_error("Voxelizer: invalid grid size");
    }
    if (config_.point_dim < 3) {
        throw std::runtime_error("Voxelizer: point_dim must be >= 3");
    }

    std::cout << "[Voxelizer] pc_range=[" << config_.pc_range[0] << ","
              << config_.pc_range[1] << "," << config_.pc_range[2] << " -> "
              << config_.pc_range[3] << "," << config_.pc_range[4] << ","
              << config_.pc_range[5] << "] voxel_size=[" << config_.voxel_size[0]
              << "," << config_.voxel_size[1] << "," << config_.voxel_size[2]
              << "] grid=" << grid_x_ << "x" << grid_y_ << "x" << grid_z_
              << " max_voxels=" << config_.max_voxels
              << " max_pts_per_voxel=" << config_.max_num_points_per_voxel
              << " point_dim=" << config_.point_dim << std::endl;

    allocate_();
}

Voxelizer::~Voxelizer() {
    try { free_(); } catch (...) {}
}

void Voxelizer::allocate_() {
    const size_t num_cells = static_cast<size_t>(grid_x_) * grid_y_ * grid_z_;
    const size_t N = config_.max_num_points_per_voxel;
    const size_t C = config_.point_dim;
    const size_t V_max = config_.max_voxels;

    pillar_buffer_ = sycl::malloc_device<float>(num_cells * N * C, queue_);
    pillar_count_  = sycl::malloc_device<int>(num_cells, queue_);

    features_   = sycl::malloc_device<float>(V_max * N * C, queue_);
    num_voxels_ = sycl::malloc_device<int>(V_max, queue_);
    coors_      = sycl::malloc_device<int>(V_max * 4, queue_);

    dev_counter_ = sycl::malloc_device<int>(1, queue_);
}

void Voxelizer::free_() {
    auto rel = [&](auto*& p) { if (p) { sycl::free(p, queue_); p = nullptr; } };
    rel(pillar_buffer_); rel(pillar_count_);
    rel(features_); rel(num_voxels_); rel(coors_);
    rel(dev_counter_);
}

int Voxelizer::run(const float* points_device, int num_points) {
    if (!points_device || num_points <= 0) {
        host_voxel_count_ = 0;
        return 0;
    }

    const size_t num_cells = static_cast<size_t>(grid_x_) * grid_y_ * grid_z_;
    const size_t N = config_.max_num_points_per_voxel;
    const size_t C = config_.point_dim;
    const size_t V_max = config_.max_voxels;

    // Reset counters and output buffers. pillar_buffer_ stores scratch slots
    // that are rewritten before compaction, while features_ stays zero-padded
    // for the static PFE input contract.
    queue_.memset(pillar_count_, 0, num_cells * sizeof(int));
    queue_.memset(dev_counter_, 0, sizeof(int));
    queue_.memset(features_, 0, V_max * N * C * sizeof(float));
    queue_.memset(num_voxels_, 0, V_max * sizeof(int));
    queue_.memset(coors_, 0, V_max * 4 * sizeof(int));

    // Kernel 1: scatter points → pillar_buffer
    const int block = 128;
    const int num_block = bevfusion::voxelizer::div_up(num_points, block);
    const int point_dim = config_.point_dim;
    const int gx = grid_x_, gy = grid_y_, gz = grid_z_;
    const float mx = config_.pc_range[0], my = config_.pc_range[1], mz = config_.pc_range[2];
    const float vx = config_.voxel_size[0], vy = config_.voxel_size[1], vz = config_.voxel_size[2];
    const int max_pts = config_.max_num_points_per_voxel;
    int* pc_ptr = pillar_count_;
    float* pb_ptr = pillar_buffer_;

    queue_.submit([&](sycl::handler& h) {
        h.parallel_for(
            sycl::nd_range<3>(
                sycl::range<3>(1, 1, num_block) * sycl::range<3>(1, 1, block),
                sycl::range<3>(1, 1, block)),
            [=](sycl::nd_item<3> item) {
                ScatterPointsKernel(points_device, num_points, point_dim,
                                    gx, gy, gz, mx, my, mz, vx, vy, vz,
                                    max_pts, pc_ptr, pb_ptr, item);
            });
    });

    // Kernel 2: compact non-empty cells → features / num_voxels / coors
    const int V_max_int = config_.max_voxels;
    int* cnt = dev_counter_;
    float* feat = features_;
    int* nv = num_voxels_;
    int* idx = coors_;

    queue_.submit([&](sycl::handler& h) {
        h.parallel_for(
            sycl::range<3>{static_cast<size_t>(gz),
                           static_cast<size_t>(gy),
                           static_cast<size_t>(gx)},
            [=](sycl::id<3> it) {
                CompactPillarsKernel(pc_ptr, pb_ptr, gx, gy,
                                     V_max_int, max_pts, point_dim,
                                     cnt, feat, nv, idx,
                                     /*x_idx=*/static_cast<int>(it[2]),
                                     /*y_idx=*/static_cast<int>(it[1]),
                                     /*z_idx=*/static_cast<int>(it[0]));
            });
    });

    int counter_value = 0;
    queue_.memcpy(&counter_value, dev_counter_, sizeof(int)).wait();
    host_voxel_count_ = (counter_value < config_.max_voxels)
                            ? counter_value : config_.max_voxels;
    return host_voxel_count_;
}

}  // namespace pointpillars
```

### lidar_voxelization/dense_ref/pointpillars/voxelizer.hpp
```
// Copyright (C) 2018-2026 Intel Corporation
// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
//
// Standalone SYCL voxelizer for PointPillars-based BEVFusion.
//
// Output tensors are locked by PillarFeatureNet.forward(features, num_voxels,
// coors) from the training repo
//   mmdet3d/models/backbones/pillar_encoder.py:188-230
//
//   features   [max_voxels, max_num_points_per_voxel, point_dim]  float32
//   num_voxels [max_voxels]                                       int32
//   coors      [max_voxels, 4]                                    int32
//              layout: (batch_idx=0, x_idx, y_idx, z_idx)
//
// IMPORTANT: coors layout is (batch, x, y, z), NOT (batch, z, y, x) like the
// CenterPoint voxelizer. This matches mmdet3d Voxelization + F.pad((1,0)).
// See bev_0422_dev.md §29.4 for the experimental verification.

#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace pointpillars {

struct VoxelizerConfig {
    // Physical extents:
    //   pc_range = [x_min, y_min, z_min, x_max, y_max, z_max]
    std::array<float, 6> pc_range    = {0.0f, -51.2f, -5.0f, 102.4f, 51.2f, 3.0f};
    std::array<float, 3> voxel_size  = {0.8f, 0.8f, 8.0f};
    int max_num_points_per_voxel      = 100;
    int max_voxels                    = 12000;
    int point_dim                     = 4;  // (x, y, z, intensity)
};

class Voxelizer {
public:
    Voxelizer(sycl::queue& queue, const VoxelizerConfig& config);
    ~Voxelizer();

    Voxelizer(const Voxelizer&) = delete;
    Voxelizer& operator=(const Voxelizer&) = delete;

    // Runs the voxelizer on a raw point cloud (device USM buffer).
    // Returns the actual number of voxels V (V <= max_voxels).
    // After this call, features_device / num_voxels_device / coors_device
    // are populated with V valid rows and zero-padded above V.
    int run(const float* points_device, int num_points);

    // Tensor accessors — names match the ONNX input tensor names so the
    // pipeline layer can bind them 1:1:
    //   req.set_tensor("features",   voxelizer.features_device_tensor());
    //   req.set_tensor("num_voxels", voxelizer.num_voxels_device_tensor());
    //   req.set_tensor("coors",      voxelizer.coors_device_tensor());
    float* features_device()  const { return features_; }   // [max_voxels, N, C]
    int*   num_voxels_device() const { return num_voxels_; } // [max_voxels]
    int*   coors_device()     const { return coors_; }       // [max_voxels, 4]

    int num_voxels() const { return host_voxel_count_; }
    int max_voxels() const { return config_.max_voxels; }
    int max_num_points_per_voxel() const { return config_.max_num_points_per_voxel; }
    int point_dim() const { return config_.point_dim; }

private:
    void allocate_();
    void free_();

    sycl::queue& queue_;
    VoxelizerConfig config_;

    int grid_x_{0}, grid_y_{0}, grid_z_{0};

    // Scratch: dense grid-sized buffers.
    //   pillar_buffer_ [num_cells, N, C]  float32 — accumulated raw points per cell
    //   pillar_count_  [num_cells]        int32   — point count per cell
    float* pillar_buffer_{nullptr};
    int*   pillar_count_{nullptr};

    // Output (compacted) buffers. All sized to max_voxels.
    float* features_{nullptr};   // [max_voxels, N, C]
    int*   num_voxels_{nullptr};  // [max_voxels]
    int*   coors_{nullptr};       // [max_voxels, 4]

    int*   dev_counter_{nullptr};  // single int — atomic sparse slot counter
    int    host_voxel_count_{0};
};

}  // namespace pointpillars
```

// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/check.hpp
// SYCL port: Assert* macros kept (pure C++). CUDA checkRuntime/check_runtime
// removed — SYCL reports errors via sycl::exception, handled by try/catch.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __CHECK_HPP__
#define __CHECK_HPP__

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

namespace nv {

#define NVUNUSED2(a, b) \
  {                     \
    (void)(a);          \
    (void)(b);          \
  }
#define NVUNUSED(a) \
  { (void)(a); }

#define Assertf(cond, fmt, ...)                                                                                         \
  do {                                                                                                                  \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                                      \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " fmt "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__); \
      abort();                                                                                                          \
    }                                                                                                                   \
  } while (false)

#define Asserts(cond, s)                                                                                 \
  do {                                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " s "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                                           \
    }                                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

#define Assert(cond)                                                                     \
  do {                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                           \
    }                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

};  // namespace nv

#endif  // #ifndef __CHECK_HPP__

### snapshots/opt1_relaxed/common/launch.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/launch.cuh
// SYCL port of the launch macros + atomics.
//   cuda_linear_index       -> it.get_global_linear_id() (handled in linear_launch)
//   cuda_linear_launch(k,s,n,...) -> linear_launch(q, n, [=](size_t idx){...})
//   atomicAdd / atomicCAS   -> atomic_fetch_add / atomic_cas below
// v1 uses seq_cst for all hash-table atomics (correctness first; relax in step 5).
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __LAUNCH_HPP__
#define __LAUNCH_HPP__

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace nv {

constexpr int LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS = 512;

inline size_t divup(size_t a, size_t b) { return (a + b - 1) / b; }

// ---- atomic helpers (device-side; operate on global USM pointers) ----
// Memory order: seq_cst by default (correctness-first v1). Compile with
// -DVOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS to switch to relaxed — a safe optimization because
// insert (build_hash_table) and lookup (voxelization) live in SEPARATE kernels
// serialized by the in-order queue + intervening D2H memcpy, so there is no
// cross-kernel insert/lookup race. Within each kernel the CAS / fetch_add only
// need atomicity (relaxed), not ordering: the CAS-winner uniquely owns the
// value-slot store, and num_points_per_voxel fetch_add returns distinct slots.
// Cross-kernel visibility of hash-table writes is guaranteed by queue ordering,
// independent of intra-kernel memory_order.
#ifdef VOX_RELAXED_ATOMICS
inline constexpr sycl::memory_order kAtomicOrder = sycl::memory_order::relaxed;
#else
inline constexpr sycl::memory_order kAtomicOrder = sycl::memory_order::seq_cst;
#endif

template <typename T>
inline T atomic_fetch_add(T* addr, T operand) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<T, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  return a.fetch_add(operand);
}

// CUDA atomicCAS(addr, compare, val) returns the OLD value.
// sycl atomic_ref::compare_exchange_strong(expected, desired) returns bool and,
// on failure, writes the actual value into `expected`. So returning `expected`
// after the call yields the old value in both success and failure cases.
inline std::uint32_t atomic_cas(std::uint32_t* addr, std::uint32_t compare, std::uint32_t val) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  std::uint32_t expected = compare;
  a.compare_exchange_strong(expected, val);
  return expected;
}

inline std::uint32_t atomic_load(std::uint32_t* addr) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  return a.load();
}

inline void atomic_store(std::uint32_t* addr, std::uint32_t val) {
  sycl::atomic_ref<std::uint32_t, kAtomicOrder, sycl::memory_scope::device,
                   sycl::access::address_space::global_space>
      a(*addr);
  a.store(val);
}

// ---- launch helper ----
// Equivalent of: cuda_linear_launch(kernel, stream, num, ...) which launches
// divup(num,512) blocks of 512 threads; the kernel receives the global 1D index
// and does its own bounds check (if (idx >= num) return;).
//
// Usage:
//   linear_launch(queue, num_points, [=](size_t idx){
//       build_hash_table_kernel(idx, num_points, points, ...);
//   });
// Returns the sycl::event for the submitted kernel so callers can use SYCL
// event profiling (requires the queue to be built with enable_profiling).
template <typename Kernel>
sycl::event linear_launch(sycl::queue& queue, size_t num, Kernel kernel) {
  if (num == 0) return sycl::event{};
  size_t blocks = divup(num, static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS));
  sycl::range<1> global{blocks * static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS)};
  sycl::range<1> local{static_cast<size_t>(LINEAR_LAUNCH_THREADS)};
  return queue.submit([&](sycl::handler& h) {
    h.parallel_for(sycl::nd_range<1>{global, local},
                   [=](sycl::nd_item<1> it) {
                     size_t idx = it.get_global_linear_id();
                     kernel(idx);
                   });
  });
}

};  // namespace nv

#endif  // __LAUNCH_HPP__
```

### snapshots/opt1_relaxed/lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.cpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/bevfusion/lidar-voxelization.cu
// SYCL port. Algorithm preserved 1:1 (hash table + scatter + reduce_mean, half, XYZ/ZYX).
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#include "lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp"
#include "common/launch.hpp"
#include "common/check.hpp"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <sycl/sycl.hpp>

namespace bevfusion {
namespace lidar {

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// device-side helpers (compiled for device when called from parallel_for)
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

static inline uint64_t hash(uint64_t k) {
  k ^= k >> 16;
  k *= 0x85ebca6b;
  k ^= k >> 13;
  k *= 0xc2b2ae35;
  k ^= k >> 16;
  return k;
}

static inline void insert_to_hash_table(const uint32_t key, uint32_t* value, const uint32_t hash_size,
                                        uint32_t* hash_table) {
  uint64_t hash_value = hash(key);
  uint32_t slot = hash_value % (hash_size / 2);  // key, value
  uint32_t empty_key = UINT32_MAX;
  while (true) {
    // CUDA: pre_key = atomicCAS(hash_table + slot, empty_key, key);
    uint32_t pre_key = nv::atomic_cas(hash_table + slot, empty_key, key);
    if (pre_key == empty_key) {
      uint32_t vid = nv::atomic_fetch_add<uint32_t>(value, 1u);
      nv::atomic_store(hash_table + slot + hash_size / 2, vid);
      break;
    } else if (pre_key == key) {
      break;
    }
    slot = (slot + 1) % (hash_size / 2);
  }
}

static inline uint32_t lookup_hash_table(const uint32_t key, const uint32_t hash_size, uint32_t* hash_table) {
  uint64_t hash_value = hash(key);
  uint32_t slot = hash_value % (hash_size / 2);  // key, value
  uint32_t empty_key = UINT32_MAX;
  while (true /* need to be adjusted according to data*/) {
    uint32_t k = nv::atomic_load(hash_table + slot);
    if (k == key) {
      return nv::atomic_load(hash_table + slot + hash_size / 2);
    } else if (k == empty_key) {
      return empty_key;
    } else {
      slot = (slot + 1) % (hash_size / 2);
    }
  }
  return empty_key;
}

template <CoordinateOrder order>
static inline void save_result_by_order(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y, unsigned int z);

template <>
inline void save_result_by_order<CoordinateOrder::XYZ>(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y,
                                                       unsigned int z) {
  *output = sycl::uint4{0, x, y, z};
}

template <>
inline void save_result_by_order<CoordinateOrder::ZYX>(sycl::uint4* output, unsigned int x, unsigned int y,
                                                       unsigned int z) {
  *output = sycl::uint4{0, z, y, x};
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// kernels (each takes the global 1D index; bounds-checks itself)
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

static inline void build_hash_table_kernel(size_t point_idx, size_t points_size, const sycl::half* points,
                                           VoxelizationParameter param, uint32_t* hash_table,
                                           uint32_t* real_voxel_num) {
  if (point_idx >= points_size) return;

  float px = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 0];
  float py = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 1];
  float pz = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 2];

  int voxel_idx = (int)sycl::floor((px - param.min_range.x) / param.voxel_size.x);
  if (voxel_idx < 0 || voxel_idx >= param.grid_size.x) return;

  int voxel_idy = (int)sycl::floor((py - param.min_range.y) / param.voxel_size.y);
  if (voxel_idy < 0 || voxel_idy >= param.grid_size.y) return;

  int voxel_idz = (int)sycl::floor((pz - param.min_range.z) / param.voxel_size.z);
  if (voxel_idz < 0 || voxel_idz >= param.grid_size.z) return;

  uint32_t voxel_offset = (voxel_idz * param.grid_size.y + voxel_idy) * param.grid_size.x + voxel_idx;
  insert_to_hash_table(voxel_offset, real_voxel_num, (uint32_t)(points_size * 2 * 2), hash_table);
}

template <CoordinateOrder order>
static inline void voxelization_kernel(size_t point_idx, size_t points_size, const sycl::half* points,
                                       VoxelizationParameter param, uint32_t* hash_table,
                                       uint32_t* num_points_per_voxel, float* voxels_temp,
                                       uint32_t* voxel_indices) {
  if (point_idx >= points_size) return;

  float px = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 0];
  float py = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 1];
  float pz = (float)points[param.num_feature * point_idx + 2];

  if (px < param.min_range.x || px >= param.max_range.x || py < param.min_range.y || py >= param.max_range.y ||
      pz < param.min_range.z || pz >= param.max_range.z) {
    return;
  }

  int voxel_idx = (int)sycl::floor((px - param.min_range.x) / param.voxel_size.x);
  int voxel_idy = (int)sycl::floor((py - param.min_range.y) / param.voxel_size.y);
  int voxel_idz = (int)sycl::floor((pz - param.min_range.z) / param.voxel_size.z);
  if (voxel_idx < 0 || voxel_idx >= param.grid_size.x) return;
  if (voxel_idy < 0 || voxel_idy >= param.grid_size.y) return;
  if (voxel_idz < 0 || voxel_idz >= param.grid_size.z) return;

  uint32_t voxel_offset = (voxel_idz * param.grid_size.y + voxel_idy) * param.grid_size.x + voxel_idx;

  uint32_t voxel_id = lookup_hash_table(voxel_offset, (uint32_t)(points_size * 2 * 2), hash_table);
  if (voxel_id >= (uint32_t)param.max_voxels) {
    return;
  }

  uint32_t current_num = nv::atomic_fetch_add<uint32_t>(num_points_per_voxel + voxel_id, 1u);
  if (current_num < (uint32_t)param.max_points_per_voxel) {
    uint32_t dst_offset =
        voxel_id * (param.num_feature * param.max_points_per_voxel) + current_num * param.num_feature;
    uint32_t src_offset = (uint32_t)point_idx * param.num_feature;
    for (int feature_idx = 0; feature_idx < param.num_feature; ++feature_idx) {
      voxels_temp[dst_offset + feature_idx] = (float)points[src_offset + feature_idx];
    }
    // Multiple points in the same voxel write the identical (x,y,z) — idempotent.
    save_result_by_order<order>((sycl::uint4*)voxel_indices + voxel_id, (unsigned int)voxel_idx,
                                (unsigned int)voxel_idy, (unsigned int)voxel_idz);
  }
}

static inline void reduce_mean_kernel(size_t voxel_idx, size_t num_voxels, float* voxels_temp,
                                      uint32_t* num_points_per_voxel, int max_points_per_voxel, int feature_num,
                                      sycl::half* voxel_features) {
  if (voxel_idx >= num_voxels) return;

  num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx] = num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx] > (uint32_t)max_points_per_voxel
                                        ? (uint32_t)max_points_per_voxel
                                        : num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx];
  int valid_points_num = (int)num_points_per_voxel[voxel_idx];
  // opt2: reduce_mean is launched over max_voxels (not the host-known real count)
  // to avoid a mid-pipeline host sync, so slots beyond the real voxel count have
  // num_points == 0 (memset) and voxels_temp == 0xff — skip them (would div-by-0).
  if (valid_points_num <= 0) return;
  int offset = voxel_idx * max_points_per_voxel * feature_num;
  for (int feature_idx = 0; feature_idx < feature_num; ++feature_idx) {
    for (int point_idx = 0; point_idx < valid_points_num - 1; ++point_idx) {
      voxels_temp[offset + feature_idx] += voxels_temp[offset + (point_idx + 1) * feature_num + feature_idx];
    }
    voxels_temp[offset + feature_idx] /= valid_points_num;
  }

  // move to be continuous
  for (int feature_idx = 0; feature_idx < feature_num; ++feature_idx) {
    int dst_offset = voxel_idx * feature_num;
    int src_offset = voxel_idx * feature_num * max_points_per_voxel;
    voxel_features[dst_offset + feature_idx] = (sycl::half)voxels_temp[src_offset + feature_idx];
  }
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// VoxelizationParameter::compute_grid_size
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

nvtype::Int3 VoxelizationParameter::compute_grid_size(const nvtype::Float3& max_range, const nvtype::Float3& min_range,
                                                      const nvtype::Float3& voxel_size) {
  nvtype::Int3 size;
  size.x = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.x - min_range.x) / voxel_size.x));
  size.y = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.y - min_range.y) / voxel_size.y));
  size.z = static_cast<int>(std::round((max_range.z - min_range.z) / voxel_size.z));
  return size;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //
// VoxelizationImplement
// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- //

// Build the internal GPU queue. Profiling is opt-in via the VOX_PROFILE env
// var: when set, enable_profiling is added so last_profile() can report per-
// kernel device time. When unset the queue is plain (no profiling overhead) —
// this keeps the B-path bench binary fair when profiling isn't requested.
static sycl::queue make_internal_queue() {
  sycl::device dev{sycl::gpu_selector_v};
  if (std::getenv("VOX_PROFILE")) {
    return sycl::queue{dev, sycl::property_list{sycl::property::queue::in_order{},
                                                 sycl::property::queue::enable_profiling{}}};
  }
  return sycl::queue{dev, sycl::property::queue::in_order{}};
}

class VoxelizationImplement : public Voxelization {
 public:
  VoxelizationImplement() {}
  virtual ~VoxelizationImplement() { free_all(); }

  bool init(VoxelizationParameter param) {
    this->param_ = param;
    this->profiling_enabled_ = (std::getenv("VOX_PROFILE") != nullptr);
    this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.x, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.z + 1};

    this->hash_table_size_ = param_.max_points * 2 * 2 * sizeof(unsigned int);
    this->voxels_temp_size_ = param_.max_voxels * param_.max_points_per_voxel * param_.num_feature * sizeof(float);
    this->voxel_features_size_ =
        param_.max_voxels * param_.max_points_per_voxel * param_.num_feature * sizeof(sycl::half);
    this->voxel_num_size_ = param_.max_voxels * sizeof(unsigned int);
    this->voxel_idxs_size_ = param_.max_voxels * 4 * sizeof(unsigned int);

    hash_table_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(hash_table_size_, queue_);
    voxels_temp_ = (float*)sycl::malloc_device(voxels_temp_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_features_ = (sycl::half*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_features_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_num_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_num_size_, queue_);
    d_voxel_indices_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(voxel_idxs_size_, queue_);
    d_real_num_voxels_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_device(sizeof(unsigned int), queue_);
    h_real_num_voxels_ = (uint32_t*)sycl::malloc_host(sizeof(unsigned int), queue_);

    if (!hash_table_ || !voxels_temp_ || !d_voxel_features_ || !d_voxel_num_ || !d_voxel_indices_ ||
        !d_real_num_voxels_ || !h_real_num_voxels_) {
      std::cerr << "[VoxelizationImplement] device/host allocation failed\n";
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  void free_all() {
    auto rel = [&](void* p) {
      if (p) sycl::free(p, queue_);
    };
    rel(hash_table_);
    rel(voxels_temp_);
    rel(d_voxel_features_);
    rel(d_voxel_num_);
    rel(d_voxel_indices_);
    rel(d_real_num_voxels_);
    rel(h_real_num_voxels_);
    hash_table_ = nullptr;
    voxels_temp_ = nullptr;
    d_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_num_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_indices_ = nullptr;
    d_voxel_features_ = nullptr;
    h_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  }

  // points and voxels must be of half type
  virtual void forward(const nvtype::half* points, int num_points, void* stream,
                       CoordinateOrder output_order) override {
    sycl::queue& q = stream ? *static_cast<sycl::queue*>(stream) : queue_;
    const sycl::half* _points = reinterpret_cast<const sycl::half*>(points);

    q.memset(hash_table_, 0xff, hash_table_size_);
    q.memset(voxels_temp_, 0xff, voxels_temp_size_);
    q.memset(d_voxel_num_, 0, voxel_num_size_);
    q.memset(d_real_num_voxels_, 0, sizeof(unsigned int));

    size_t points_size = (size_t)num_points;

    // SYCL device lambdas cannot implicitly capture `this`; copy members by value.
    auto param = param_;
    uint32_t* hash_table = hash_table_;
    uint32_t* d_real_num_voxels = d_real_num_voxels_;
    uint32_t* d_voxel_num = d_voxel_num_;
    float* voxels_temp = voxels_temp_;
    uint32_t* d_voxel_indices = d_voxel_indices_;
    sycl::half* d_voxel_features = d_voxel_features_;

    ev_build_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
      build_hash_table_kernel(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_real_num_voxels);
    });

    // D2H of real voxel count (async, ordered after build_hash_table in in-order queue)
    q.memcpy(h_real_num_voxels_, d_real_num_voxels_, sizeof(int));

    if (output_order == CoordinateOrder::XYZ) {
      ev_vox_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
        voxelization_kernel<CoordinateOrder::XYZ>(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_voxel_num,
                                                   voxels_temp, d_voxel_indices);
      });
      this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.x, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.z + 1};
    } else if (output_order == CoordinateOrder::ZYX) {
      ev_vox_ = nv::linear_launch(q, points_size, [=](size_t idx) {
        voxelization_kernel<CoordinateOrder::ZYX>(idx, points_size, _points, param, hash_table, d_voxel_num,
                                                   voxels_temp, d_voxel_indices);
      });
      this->output_grid_size_ = {(int)param_.grid_size.z + 1, (int)param_.grid_size.y, (int)param_.grid_size.x};
    } else {
      Assertf(false, "Invalid output_order: %d", static_cast<int>(output_order));
    }

    q.wait();  // == cudaStreamSynchronize (waits for the D2H memcpy too)
    real_num_voxels_ = *h_real_num_voxels_;

    auto num_voxels = real_num_voxels_;
    int max_points_per_voxel = param_.max_points_per_voxel;
    int num_feature = param_.num_feature;
    ev_reduce_ = nv::linear_launch(q, (size_t)num_voxels, [=](size_t idx) {
      reduce_mean_kernel(idx, (size_t)num_voxels, voxels_temp, d_voxel_num, max_points_per_voxel, num_feature,
                         d_voxel_features);
    });
  }

  virtual void* internal_stream() override { return &queue_; }

  virtual bool last_profile(uint64_t& build_hash_ns, uint64_t& voxelization_ns,
                            uint64_t& reduce_mean_ns) override {
    if (!profiling_enabled_) return false;
    auto dur_ns = [](const sycl::event& e) -> uint64_t {
      // A default-constructed / empty event (e.g. linear_launch with num==0)
      // has no profiling info — get_profiling_info throws, so guard it.
      try {
        uint64_t s = e.get_profiling_info<sycl::info::event_profiling::command_start>();
        uint64_t en = e.get_profiling_info<sycl::info::event_profiling::command_end>();
        return en - s;
      } catch (...) {
        return 0;
      }
    };
    build_hash_ns = dur_ns(ev_build_);
    voxelization_ns = dur_ns(ev_vox_);
    reduce_mean_ns = dur_ns(ev_reduce_);
    return true;
  }

  virtual unsigned int num_voxels() override { return real_num_voxels_; }
  virtual unsigned int voxel_dim() override { return param_.num_feature; }
  virtual unsigned int indices_dim() override { return 4; }
  virtual std::vector<int> grid_size() override { return output_grid_size_; }
  virtual const void* indices() override { return d_voxel_indices_; }
  virtual const void* features() override { return d_voxel_features_; }
  virtual CoordinateOrder order() override { return order_; }

 private:
  sycl::queue queue_{make_internal_queue()};

  CoordinateOrder order_ = CoordinateOrder::NoneOrder;
  VoxelizationParameter param_;
  unsigned int real_num_voxels_ = 0;
  std::vector<int> output_grid_size_;
  bool profiling_enabled_ = false;

  // Profiling events from the most recent forward() (valid only if profiling_enabled_).
  sycl::event ev_build_;
  sycl::event ev_vox_;
  sycl::event ev_reduce_;

  uint32_t* hash_table_ = nullptr;
  float* voxels_temp_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* h_real_num_voxels_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_voxel_num_ = nullptr;
  sycl::half* d_voxel_features_ = nullptr;
  uint32_t* d_voxel_indices_ = nullptr;
  size_t hash_table_size_;
  size_t voxels_temp_size_;
  size_t voxel_features_size_;
  size_t voxel_idxs_size_;
  size_t voxel_num_size_;
};

std::shared_ptr<Voxelization> create_voxelization(VoxelizationParameter param) {
  std::shared_ptr<VoxelizationImplement> impl(new VoxelizationImplement());
  if (!impl->init(param)) {
    impl.reset();
  }
  return impl;
}

};  // namespace lidar
};  // namespace bevfusion
```

### snapshots/opt1_relaxed/lidar_voxelization/voxelizer.hpp
```
// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/bevfusion/lidar-voxelization.hpp
// SYCL port of the voxelization interface. Algorithm/semantics unchanged.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__
#define __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "common/dtype.hpp"

namespace bevfusion {
namespace lidar {

enum class CoordinateOrder : int {
  NoneOrder = 0,
  XYZ = 1,  // BEVFusion
  ZYX = 2   // CenterPoint
};

struct VoxelizationParameter {
  nvtype::Float3 min_range;
  nvtype::Float3 max_range;
  nvtype::Float3 voxel_size;
  nvtype::Int3 grid_size;
  int num_feature;
  int max_voxels;
  int max_points_per_voxel;
  int max_points;

  static nvtype::Int3 compute_grid_size(const nvtype::Float3& max_range, const nvtype::Float3& min_range,
                                        const nvtype::Float3& voxel_size);
};

class Voxelization {
 public:
  virtual ~Voxelization() = default;
  // points and voxels must be of half-float device pointer.
  // stream is a sycl::queue* (nullptr => use the implementation's internal queue).
  virtual void forward(const nvtype::half* points, int num_points, void* stream = nullptr,
                       CoordinateOrder output_order = CoordinateOrder::XYZ) = 0;

  virtual unsigned int num_voxels() = 0;
  virtual unsigned int voxel_dim() = 0;
  virtual unsigned int indices_dim() = 0;
  virtual std::vector<int> grid_size() = 0;
  virtual const void* indices() = 0;
  virtual const void* features() = 0;
  virtual CoordinateOrder order() = 0;

  // Returns the implementation's internal sycl::queue* so callers can allocate
  // USM in the SAME context (cast the void* to sycl::queue*).
  virtual void* internal_stream() = 0;

  // Per-kernel device profiling from the most recent forward() call.
  // Returns true if profiling was enabled (queue built with enable_profiling,
  // i.e. VOX_PROFILE env var was set at construction) and a run has completed;
  // the three out-params are filled with each kernel's device execution time in
  // nanoseconds. Returns false otherwise (out-params untouched). The caller must
  // ensure the queue is idle (e.g. q.wait()) before calling.
  virtual bool last_profile(uint64_t& build_hash_ns, uint64_t& voxelization_ns,
                            uint64_t& reduce_mean_ns) = 0;
};

std::shared_ptr<Voxelization> create_voxelization(VoxelizationParameter parameter);

};  // namespace lidar
};  // namespace bevfusion

#endif  // __LIDAR_VOXELIZATION_HPP__
```

// Ported from NVIDIA Lidar_AI_Solution CUDA-BEVFusion src/common/check.hpp
// SYCL port: Assert* macros kept (pure C++). CUDA checkRuntime/check_runtime
// removed — SYCL reports errors via sycl::exception, handled by try/catch.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT (NVIDIA original)

#ifndef __CHECK_HPP__
#define __CHECK_HPP__

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

namespace nv {

#define NVUNUSED2(a, b) \
  {                     \
    (void)(a);          \
    (void)(b);          \
  }
#define NVUNUSED(a) \
  { (void)(a); }

#define Assertf(cond, fmt, ...)                                                                                         \
  do {                                                                                                                  \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                                      \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " fmt "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__, __VA_ARGS__); \
      abort();                                                                                                          \
    }                                                                                                                   \
  } while (false)

#define Asserts(cond, s)                                                                                 \
  do {                                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d, message: " s "\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                                           \
    }                                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

#define Assert(cond)                                                                     \
  do {                                                                                   \
    if (!(cond)) {                                                                       \
      fprintf(stderr, "Assert failed. %s in file %s:%d\n", #cond, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
      abort();                                                                           \
    }                                                                                    \
  } while (false)

};  // namespace nv

#endif  // #ifndef __CHECK_HPP__
